# @reftrixmcp/mcp-server Changelog / 変更履歴

and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## [Unreleased] / [Unreleased]

### Added / 追加

- **`publish-yml-sigpipe-guard.test.ts` — the H-closure test for the `publish.yml` `tar`-pipe SIGPIPE guard (package-level; internal test; branch `feature/npm-publish-automation`; IO Impl Decision V1 = APPROVE `019f5346-7542-7243-9e7a-bc57376f3d8d`) — 2026-07-12** / **`publish-yml-sigpipe-guard.test.ts` — `publish.yml` の `tar`-pipe SIGPIPE guard に対する H-closure テスト（パッケージレベル; 内部テスト）— 2026-07-12**:
  - **EN — Added**: New test `apps/mcp-server/tests/scripts/publish-yml-sigpipe-guard.test.ts` (253 lines, 7 tests) — the code + CI-failing test landing that closed FIND-NPMPUB-VERIFY-SIGPIPE-01 (H). Two verification layers: (i) a **source-pin sweep** over `.github/workflows/publish.yml` asserting that every `tar … | grep` pipe in the `verify` job is wrapped in the `(tar … 2>/dev/null || true) | grep …` SIGPIPE-absorbing pattern (so `grep -q`'s early pipe-close on a large tarball listing — `apps/mcp-server`'s ~2600 files, ~150 KB > the 64 KB pipe buffer — cannot make `tar` receive SIGPIPE/exit 141 and surface a false failure under GitHub Actions' default `-eo pipefail`); (ii) a **synthetic-tarball fail-closed check** that a genuinely non-conforming tarball still fails the assertion (the guard absorbs the SIGPIPE, not the assertion). This test lives in the `apps/mcp-server` package but validates the OSS-synced `publish.yml` (whose changelog entry is in the root + internal CHANGELOGs); it is deterministic (IO-run 7/7 passed, ~230 ms).
  - **JP — 追加**: 新テスト `apps/mcp-server/tests/scripts/publish-yml-sigpipe-guard.test.ts`（253 行、7 tests）— FIND-NPMPUB-VERIFY-SIGPIPE-01 (H) を closure した code + CI-failing test landing。2 層検証: (i) `.github/workflows/publish.yml` に対する **source-pin sweep** で、`verify` job の全 `tar … | grep` pipe が `(tar … 2>/dev/null || true) | grep …` SIGPIPE 吸収パターンで wrap されていることを assert（大きな tarball listing — `apps/mcp-server` の ~2600 file、~150 KB > 64 KB pipe buffer — に対する `grep -q` の早期 pipe-close で `tar` が SIGPIPE/exit 141 を受け GitHub Actions 既定 `-eo pipefail` 下で false failure として surface することを構造的に防ぐ）; (ii) **合成 tarball fail-closed チェック**で、真に非準拠な tarball が依然 assertion を fail することを検証（guard は SIGPIPE を吸収するが assertion は吸収しない）。本テストは `apps/mcp-server` パッケージ内に置かれるが OSS-synced な `publish.yml`（その changelog エントリは root + 内部 CHANGELOG）を検証する; 決定論的（IO 実行 7/7 passed、~230 ms）。
  - **EN — Env var / DB migration / MCP tool / audit action / enum**: none — a new internal script test only (no MCP tool I/O schema surface; `gen:tool-count` unchanged at 40; NOT in INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 scope; no migration; no env var; no audit action). AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-07-12.
  - **JP — Env var / DB migration / MCP tool / audit action / enum**: なし — 新規の内部 script テストのみ（MCP tool I/O schema に非 surface; `gen:tool-count` は 40 のまま; INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 scope 外; migration なし; env var なし; audit action なし）。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-07-12。

### Removed / 削除

- **Removed the vestigial mock `api/health.ts` HTTP endpoint (dead code) + version-coherence test alignment (package-level; OSS-synced production source; branch `chore/l-tracked-post-release-bundle`; IO Impl Decision = CONDITIONAL / documentation-only `019f5138-a188-743f-8ed6-9195573b2ecb`) — 2026-07-11** / **未配線のモック `api/health.ts` HTTP エンドポイント（dead code）の削除 + version-coherence テスト整合（パッケージレベル; OSS-synced production source; branch `chore/l-tracked-post-release-bundle`; IO Impl Decision = CONDITIONAL / documentation-only `019f5138-a188-743f-8ed6-9195573b2ecb`）— 2026-07-11**:
  - **EN — Removed**: Deleted the `api/health.ts` mock health endpoint file (**−375 lines**): a `HealthCheckService` class + a `createHealthCheckEndpoint` factory + a default export, all **dead code** with zero construction sites in `apps/mcp-server/src` (never instantiated by the running MCP server). It returned hardcoded mock component versions from an unreachable endpoint (mock DB `"18.1"` / Redis `"7.2"`), so its removal shrinks a stale-mock CWE-209-adjacent information-exposure surface. The real health surface — the `system.health` MCP tool — is a separate, independently-wired path and is **UNCHANGED** (`gen:tool-count` unchanged at 40; no MCP tool I/O change).
  - **EN — Tests**: `server-version-coherence.test.ts` (~97 lines changed) — removed the now-orphaned `HealthCheckService` leg; the `SERVER_CONFIG.version === package.json.version` coherence assertion (the load-bearing pin from the v0.6.0 stale-drift fix) stays intact. `server.test.ts` (~29 lines changed) — removed a vacuous placeholder version test (a Green-unreached `expect(server).toBeDefined()` shell that asserted nothing about the version); the 5 remaining placeholder tests (name/capabilities/connect×2/close) are pre-existing tech debt tracked as TDA-BUNDLE-L-01 (deadline 2026-07-12).
  - **EN — Env var / DB migration / MCP tool / audit action / enum**: none — internal dead-code removal only (no MCP tool I/O schema surface; `gen:tool-count` unchanged at 40; NOT in INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 scope; no migration, no env var, no audit action). AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-07-11.
  - **JP — 削除**: `api/health.ts` モック health エンドポイントファイル（**−375 行**）を削除: `HealthCheckService` クラス + `createHealthCheckEndpoint` ファクトリ + default export はいずれも現行 `apps/mcp-server/src` 内に construction site が 0 件の **dead code**（稼働中の MCP サーバーが一切 instantiate しない）。到達不能なエンドポイントからハードコードされたモックのコンポーネントバージョン（モック DB `"18.1"` / Redis `"7.2"`）を返していたため、削除により stale なモックの CWE-209 隣接な情報露出 surface を縮小する。実際の health surface である `system.health` MCP ツールは独立に配線された別経路であり **無改変**（`gen:tool-count` は 40 のまま; MCP tool I/O 変更なし）。
  - **JP — テスト**: `server-version-coherence.test.ts`（~97 行変更）— orphan 化した `HealthCheckService` leg を撤去; `SERVER_CONFIG.version === package.json.version` coherence assertion（v0.6.0 の stale-drift 修正由来の load-bearing pin）は維持。`server.test.ts`（~29 行変更）— 空虚な placeholder version テスト（バージョンについて何も assert しない Green 未到達の `expect(server).toBeDefined()` シェル）を撤去; 残る 5 件の placeholder テスト（name/capabilities/connect×2/close）は pre-existing tech debt で TDA-BUNDLE-L-01 として tracked（deadline 2026-07-12）。
  - **JP — Env var / DB migration / MCP tool / audit action / enum**: なし — 内部 dead-code 除去のみ（MCP tool I/O schema に非 surface; `gen:tool-count` は 40 のまま; INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 scope 外; migration なし、env var なし、audit action なし）。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-07-11。

## [0.6.0] — 2026-07-11 / [0.6.0] — 2026-07-11

### Added / 追加

- **Cross-page section gallery `scope` content-first param + `facets` chip counts (`GET /internal/sections`) (package-level; OSS-synced production source; NOT an MCP tool; additive backward-compatible) — working-tree (branch `feature/webui`; WebUI v1 W7c-api-2; follow-up to W7c-api main commit `893510b2`; IO Impl Decision = APPROVE `019f444e-f874-7148-8815-7e9b1d467dac`; 2026-07-09; T+1d 2026-07-10)** / **全ページ横断セクションギャラリー `scope` content-first param + `facets` チップ集計（`GET /internal/sections`）（パッケージレベル; OSS-synced production source; MCP ツールではない; additive 後方互換）**:
  - **EN — Added**: Additively extended `getGallerySections` (`gallery.service.ts`, READ-ONLY, zero ML). (1) **`scope` param** — a new `scope: GalleryScope = "all"` 4th argument threaded from `handleGallerySections` (`server.ts`); `galleryQuerySchema` gains `scope: z.enum(["all", "content"]).default("all")` (`schemas.ts:119`). The default `"all"` leaves the count/findMany `where` byte-identical to W7c-api; `if (scope === "content" && type === undefined) { where.sectionType = { notIn: [...CHROME_SECTION_TYPES] }; }` (`gallery.service.ts:156`) is the ONLY mutation — a const-object property mutation ruled VALID (the only solution satisfying all 4 existing AST source-pins at `:95-99`; the `type===undefined` gate guarantees explicit-`type` precedence; count/findMany still share the same `where` object). `CHROME_SECTION_TYPES = ["navigation", "footer", "unknown"] as const` (`gallery.service.ts:52`) is the exported SSOT (never inlined at a call site). New exported `GalleryScope = "all" | "content"` TS union (Zod-bridged at the route seam). (2) **`facets`** — `GalleryResponse` gains `facets: GalleryFacet[]` (`GalleryFacet = { sectionType: string; count: number }`); a 3rd `Promise.all` leg `prisma.sectionPattern.groupBy({ by: ["sectionType"], where: buildGalleryWhere(undefined), _count: { _all: true } })` (`gallery.service.ts:190`) — `buildGalleryWhere(undefined)` reuses the EXACT crop-bearing + high-PII belt (no `type`/`scope`), so a high-PII section never enters a facet count; the result is mapped to `{ sectionType, count: _count._all }` and sorted count-descending. `Σfacets === total` holds only for a `scope="all"`/no-`type` request (identical `where`, JSDoc-noted). **L tracked-issues (deadline 2026-07-10)**: TPA-IMPL-W7CAPI2-1 (content-first is an API capability only; UI realization → W7c-ui **F-1 binding hand-off**, owner frontend-engineer + backend-api-developer) / TPA-2≡TDA-2 (facets recompute per-page — page-invariant redundant, Promise.all parallel = zero added latency, bounded corpus + localhost acceptable) / TPA-4≡TDA-3 (where const-object mutation informational — declarative form when the 4 pins relax) / SEC-IMPL-W7CAPI2-1 (facets groupBy O(corpus) per request — user-uncontrolled + 120 RPM + localhost → no DoS, perf domain) / SEC-IMPL-W7CAPI2-2 (`Σfacets=total` scope-conditional invariant, JSDoc-noted) / TDA-IMPL-W7CAPI2-1 (`gallery.service.ts` outside the ESLint scoped complexity override — CC measured 3, existing L-class convention) / LCC-IMPL-W7CAPI2-3 (ADR-0042 Amendment 13 §A13.1 scope/facets sync — landed this Phase 3). AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-07-09.
  - **JP — 追加**: `getGallerySections`（`gallery.service.ts`、READ-ONLY、zero ML）を additive に拡張。(1) **`scope` param** — `handleGallerySections`（`server.ts`）から thread する新 4 番目引数 `scope: GalleryScope = "all"`; `galleryQuerySchema` に `scope: z.enum(["all", "content"]).default("all")`（`schemas.ts:119`）を追加。default `"all"` は count/findMany の `where` を W7c-api と byte-identical に保つ; `if (scope === "content" && type === undefined) { where.sectionType = { notIn: [...CHROME_SECTION_TYPES] }; }`（`gallery.service.ts:156`）が唯一の mutation — const-object property mutation は VALID 裁定（既存 4 AST source-pin（`:95-99`）を同時充足する唯一解; `type===undefined` gate が explicit-`type` precedence を保証; count/findMany は依然同一 `where` object を共有）。`CHROME_SECTION_TYPES = ["navigation", "footer", "unknown"] as const`（`gallery.service.ts:52`）は export された SSOT（call site で inline 化しない）。新 export `GalleryScope = "all" | "content"` TS union（route seam で Zod-bridge）。(2) **`facets`** — `GalleryResponse` に `facets: GalleryFacet[]`（`GalleryFacet = { sectionType: string; count: number }`）を追加; 3 番目の `Promise.all` leg `prisma.sectionPattern.groupBy({ by: ["sectionType"], where: buildGalleryWhere(undefined), _count: { _all: true } })`（`gallery.service.ts:190`）— `buildGalleryWhere(undefined)` は crop-bearing + high-PII belt を厳密再利用（`type`/`scope` なし）ゆえ high-PII section は facet count に混入しない; 結果を `{ sectionType, count: _count._all }` に map し count 降順 sort。`Σfacets === total` は `scope="all"`/no-`type` リクエストに限り成立（同一 `where`、JSDoc 明記）。**L tracked-issues（deadline 2026-07-10）**: TPA-IMPL-W7CAPI2-1（content-first は API capability のみ; UI 実現 → W7c-ui **F-1 binding hand-off**、owner frontend-engineer + backend-api-developer）/ TPA-2≡TDA-2（facets の per-page 再計算 — page-invariant で冗長、Promise.all 並列 = 追加 latency ゼロ、bounded corpus + localhost で許容）/ TPA-4≡TDA-3（where const-object mutation informational — 4 pin 緩和時に宣言形へ）/ SEC-IMPL-W7CAPI2-1（facets groupBy が per-request O(corpus) — user 非制御 + 120 RPM + localhost → DoS 不成立、perf 領域）/ SEC-IMPL-W7CAPI2-2（`Σfacets=total` は scope 条件付き不変条件、JSDoc 明記）/ TDA-IMPL-W7CAPI2-1（`gallery.service.ts` は ESLint scoped complexity override 対象外 — CC 実測 3、既存 L-class 慣例）/ LCC-IMPL-W7CAPI2-3（ADR-0042 Amendment 13 §A13.1 scope/facets sync — 本 Phase 3 で landing）。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-07-09。
  - **Per-file change summary / ファイル別変更サマリ**: `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/schemas.ts` (+9 — `galleryQuerySchema.scope` enum + JSDoc) / `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/gallery.service.ts` (+57 −11 — `CHROME_SECTION_TYPES` SSOT :52 + `GalleryScope` type + `GalleryFacet` interface + `GalleryResponse.facets` + scope `notIn` gate :156 + `groupBy` facets :190 + count-desc sort) / `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/server.ts` (+19 −5 — `handleGallerySections` scope threading). Tests: `apps/mcp-server/tests/api/internal/gallery-schema.test.ts` (`INV-WEBUI-GALLERY-QUERY-BOUND`, no-DB, **11** tests incl. `scope` enum reject) + `apps/mcp-server/tests/integration/gallery-pii-excluded.integration.test.ts` (`INV-WEBUI-GALLERY-PII-EXCLUDED`, real-DB pass^3, **18** tests incl. facets belt-share legs d1/d2/d3 + scope legs a/b). Total diff **322 ins / 13 del** (5 files; feat-only +89 / −12).
  - **Env var / DB migration / MCP tool / audit action / enum**: none — an additive extension of an existing internal read HTTP route (no MCP tool I/O schema surface; `gen:tool-count` unchanged at 40; NOT in INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 scope; no migration; no env var; no audit action — the gallery listing is a read aggregation, LCC-P05 preserved). / なし — 既存 internal read HTTP route の additive 拡張（MCP tool I/O schema に非 surface; `gen:tool-count` は 40 のまま; INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 scope 外; migration なし; env var なし; audit action なし — gallery listing は read aggregation、LCC-P05 維持）。
- **Cross-page section gallery read endpoint (`GET /internal/sections`) (package-level; OSS-synced production source; NOT an MCP tool) — working-tree (branch `feature/webui`; WebUI v1 W7c-api; IO Impl Decision V0 = CONDITIONAL `019f43f1-7b14-7459-80a6-09a572f303a9` → V1 = APPROVE `019f4403-e79f-7358-8b16-2ab90eea9b3a`; 2026-07-09; T+1d 2026-07-10)** / **全ページ横断セクションギャラリー read エンドポイント（`GET /internal/sections`）（パッケージレベル; OSS-synced production source; MCP ツールではない）**:
  - **EN — Added**: New read-only internal read-API route `GET /internal/sections?type=&page=&pageSize=` (`server.ts` `handleGallerySections` + `internalRateLimit("internal_sections")`) for the WebUI cross-page section gallery. `getGallerySections(page, pageSize, type)` (`gallery.service.ts`, NEW, READ-ONLY, zero ML): a single `buildGalleryWhere(type)` returns the `where` shared by `prisma.sectionPattern.count` + `.findMany` (count/items symmetry) — `embedding.cropStoragePath: { not: null }` (crop-bearing population, structural high-PII exclusion) + `componentParts: { none: { piiRiskLevel: HIGH_PII } }` (belt-and-braces READ-sink; `HIGH_PII` imported from `page-detail.service`, 0 inline literal) + the optional `sectionType` filter; deterministic `orderBy` (`webPage.crawledAt desc` → `webPageId` → `positionIndex` → `id`); `skip`/`take` offset pagination. `getLatestQualityByPageIds` is called ONCE over the de-duped `webPageId` set (no N+1). `GallerySectionItem` = 7 fields, `htmlSnippet`/`attributes`/`cssClasses` intentionally omitted (0 markup sink). `galleryQuerySchema` + `GALLERY_SECTION_TYPE_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9 _-]{0,49}$/` in `schemas.ts` (pageSize ≤ 100 CWE-770 + allowlist regex, caller default pageSize 24). `TOOL_TIER_MAP.internal_sections = "search"` in `rate-limiter.ts` (120 RPM, shared Token Bucket core). `gen:tool-count` unchanged at 40 (not an MCP tool). Standing INV: `INV-WEBUI-GALLERY-PII-EXCLUDED` (`tests/integration/gallery-pii-excluded.integration.test.ts`, real-DB, 9 tests — a corpus high-PII=0 leg + a `data.delete`→gallery-0 leg, relocated into the DB-bearing `test-integration` CI job) + `INV-WEBUI-GALLERY-QUERY-BOUND` (`tests/api/internal/gallery-schema.test.ts`, no-DB, 8 tests) = 17 tests. ADR-0042 Amendment 13. **L tracked-issues (deadline 2026-07-10)**: SEC-IMPL-W7CAPI-1 (`pagesQuerySchema.page` unbounded — pre-existing, shared with `/internal/pages`・`parts`, mitigated by `take ≤ 100` + localhost dogfood + 120 RPM) / TDA-IMPL-W7CAPI-1 (`GallerySectionItem` webui↔mcp-server hand-mirror parity test → W7c-ui, Carryover TDA-P03) / TDA-IMPL-W7CAPI-2 (`gallery.service.ts` outside the ESLint scoped complexity override — CC 2-3, no harm) / LCC-IMPL-W7CAPI-2 (`MAX_PAGES` corpus-sweep cap) / TPA-IMPL-W7CAPI-2 (`count`/`findMany` non-txn momentary inconsistency — PII implication ZERO, documented accepted-risk, not-recommended-fix). AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-07-09.
  - **JP — 追加**: WebUI の全ページ横断セクションギャラリー用に read-only 内部 read-API route `GET /internal/sections?type=&page=&pageSize=`（`server.ts` `handleGallerySections` + `internalRateLimit("internal_sections")`）を追加。`getGallerySections(page, pageSize, type)`（`gallery.service.ts`、NEW、READ-ONLY、zero ML）: 単一 `buildGalleryWhere(type)` が返す `where` を `prisma.sectionPattern.count` + `.findMany` で共有（count/items 対称）— `embedding.cropStoragePath: { not: null }`（crop-bearing 母集団、structural high-PII 除外）+ `componentParts: { none: { piiRiskLevel: HIGH_PII } }`（belt-and-braces READ-sink; `HIGH_PII` は `page-detail.service` import、inline literal 0）+ optional `sectionType` filter; 決定論的 `orderBy`（`webPage.crawledAt desc` → `webPageId` → `positionIndex` → `id`）; `skip`/`take` offset pagination。`getLatestQualityByPageIds` は de-dup した `webPageId` 集合に対し **1 回** call（N+1 なし）。`GallerySectionItem` = 7 field、`htmlSnippet`/`attributes`/`cssClasses` は意図的に非返却（markup sink 0）。`schemas.ts` に `galleryQuerySchema` + `GALLERY_SECTION_TYPE_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9 _-]{0,49}$/`（pageSize ≤ 100 CWE-770 + allowlist regex、caller default pageSize 24）。`rate-limiter.ts` に `TOOL_TIER_MAP.internal_sections = "search"`（120 RPM、shared Token Bucket core）。`gen:tool-count` は 40 のまま（MCP tool ではない）。Standing INV: `INV-WEBUI-GALLERY-PII-EXCLUDED`（`tests/integration/gallery-pii-excluded.integration.test.ts`、real-DB、9 tests — corpus high-PII=0 leg + `data.delete`→gallery-0 leg、DB を持つ `test-integration` CI job へ移設）+ `INV-WEBUI-GALLERY-QUERY-BOUND`（`tests/api/internal/gallery-schema.test.ts`、no-DB、8 tests）= 17 tests。ADR-0042 Amendment 13。**L tracked-issues（deadline 2026-07-10）**: SEC-IMPL-W7CAPI-1（`pagesQuerySchema.page` 上限なし — pre-existing、`/internal/pages`・`parts` と共有、`take ≤ 100` + localhost dogfood + 120 RPM で緩和）/ TDA-IMPL-W7CAPI-1（`GallerySectionItem` webui↔mcp-server hand-mirror parity test → W7c-ui、Carryover TDA-P03）/ TDA-IMPL-W7CAPI-2（`gallery.service.ts` は ESLint scoped complexity override 対象外 — CC 2-3、実害なし）/ LCC-IMPL-W7CAPI-2（`MAX_PAGES` corpus-sweep cap）/ TPA-IMPL-W7CAPI-2（`count`/`findMany` non-txn 瞬間不整合 — PII 含意ゼロ、documented accepted-risk、not-recommended-fix）。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-07-09。

- **Part bbox `__name` ReferenceError fix — decompose the `page.evaluate` body into argument-position anonymous callbacks (package-level; OSS-synced production source; internal-only, no MCP tool I/O change) — working-tree (branch `feature/webui`; WebUI v1 W6 Issue A `__name` follow-up to PR-2; IO Plan Decision V1 = APPROVE / IO Impl Decision = APPROVE; 2026-07-01; T+1d 2026-07-02)** / **Part bbox `__name` ReferenceError 修正 — `page.evaluate` body を argument-position anonymous callback に分解（パッケージレベル; OSS-synced production source; internal-only、MCP tool I/O 変更なし）**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: The W6 Issue A PR-2 bbox gate-fix (`ad027701`) had decomposed the browser-context `page.evaluate` body of `runBboxPageEvaluate` (`part-bbox-playwright.service.ts`) into **named arrow `const` helpers** (`finalize` / `matchInContainer` / `resolveOne`). Under the tsx (esbuild) `keepNames` transform a named binding becomes `const finalize = __name((...) => {}, "finalize")`, and `page.evaluate` serializes its callback via `Function.prototype.toString()`, so the `__name(...)` helper call leaked into the browser (where `__name` is undefined) → `ReferenceError: __name is not defined` on every page → the throw was swallowed by the sweep catch and sanitised → `resolvedCount: 0` everywhere (all deep-part bbox resolution lost). The fix re-decomposes the body into **argument-position anonymous callbacks only** (`data.map((part) => ...)` + `[selector].map((selector) => ...)[0]`), which esbuild `keepNames` never wraps, so the serialized body emits no `__name(...)`. **Decomposition method = Candidate B** (max cyclomatic complexity **9**, headroom +1 under the `complexity:["error",10]` scoped override; `data.map` CC=6 / `[selector].map` CC=9), chosen over Candidate A (CC=10, headroom 0) because this callback is the browser-context MIRROR of the Node SSOT `resolvePartBboxesInScope` (a churn-prone parity hotspot) — **no `eslint-disable` escape hatch** (the decomposition met CC≤10 empirically, verified by 3 independent measurements: IO lint 0 + TPA `npx eslint` exit 0 + TDA CC re-measure max=9). Semantic equivalence is byte-level SOUND (TPA): the 3-state return / shared `matched` Set / `container` single-assignment narrowing / the 11-literal clamp-edge / population-divergent / honest-skip parity are all preserved, and `INV-TOLERANCE-NON-GATING-001` (Layer (i) parity regex + Layer (ii)/(iii) Node SSOT) stays GREEN, untouched. **New standing regression** `INV-PAGE-EVALUATE-NO-NAME-INJECTION-001` (H, large-page, transform-level: esbuild + ts-morph over production source) asserts zero `__name(...)` in the callback **body span** (L-11: the count is scoped to the `page.evaluate` argument-callback body, NOT the whole transformed module — the outer `runBboxPageEvaluate` keepNames wrap and ~16 unrelated wraps must be excluded or a clean fix false-REDs) with a non-vacuity 2-guard (M-3: a named-bound positive-control fixture MUST emit `__name`; an AST locator-guard MUST find ≥1 callback) + a tsx provenance guard (L-3: the `keepNames` + `minifyWhitespace` opts are pinned to the installed tsx's frozen transform options). It lands Tier1 (`runBboxPageEvaluate`) only — a codebase-wide Tier2 scan is deferred to a follow-up PR because PRE-step 2 found additional tsx-reachable unsafe `page.evaluate` bodies in the motion/animation detectors (out of THIS PR's scope; an allowlist-free Tier2 INV would land standing-RED P0). **IO ground-truth**: typecheck 0 / lint 0 / format:check clean / docs:verify 53 PASS / FULL standing 4-domain 138 files・956 tests passed・0 failed; the new INV file = vitest 26 passed. **Real-machine DoD** (fresh `page.analyze` of stripe.com via the tsx production-canonical worker): sectionSelector 0→14 + `resolvedCount` 0→18 + the `page.evaluate() failed during scroll sweep` warn disappeared — proving the fix makes bbox resolution actually function. AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-07-01.
  - **JP — 修正**: W6 Issue A PR-2 bbox gate-fix（`ad027701`）が `runBboxPageEvaluate`（`part-bbox-playwright.service.ts`）の browser-context `page.evaluate` body を **named arrow `const` helper**（`finalize` / `matchInContainer` / `resolveOne`）に分解していた。tsx（esbuild）`keepNames` transform 下で named binding は `const finalize = __name((...) => {}, "finalize")` になり、`page.evaluate` が callback を `Function.prototype.toString()` でシリアライズするため `__name(...)` helper call が browser（`__name` 未定義）に混入 → 毎ページ `ReferenceError: __name is not defined` → throw は sweep catch で握られ sanitise され `resolvedCount: 0`（深部 part bbox 解決が全損）。修正は body を **argument-position の anonymous callback のみ**（`data.map((part) => ...)` + `[selector].map((selector) => ...)[0]`）に再分解する（esbuild `keepNames` は引数 arrow を wrap しない）ため、シリアライズ body は `__name(...)` を emit しない。**分解方式 = Candidate B**（max cyclomatic complexity **9**、`complexity:["error",10]` scoped override 下で headroom +1; `data.map` CC=6 / `[selector].map` CC=9）を Candidate A（CC=10、headroom 0）より採用 — 当該 callback は Node SSOT `resolvePartBboxesInScope` の browser-context MIRROR（churn-prone な parity hotspot）であるため。**`eslint-disable` escape hatch なし**（分解が CC≤10 を実証的に満たす、3 独立計測で確認: IO lint 0 + TPA `npx eslint` exit 0 + TDA CC 再計測 max=9）。semantic 等価は byte-level SOUND（TPA）: 3-state return / shared `matched` Set / `container` single-assignment narrowing / 11-literal の clamp-edge / population-divergent / honest-skip parity を全て保存し、`INV-TOLERANCE-NON-GATING-001`（Layer (i) parity regex + Layer (ii)/(iii) Node SSOT）は GREEN・無改変。**新 standing regression** `INV-PAGE-EVALUATE-NO-NAME-INJECTION-001`（H、large-page、transform レベル: production source に esbuild + ts-morph）が callback **body span** 内の `__name(...)` 0 件を assert する（L-11: count は `page.evaluate` 引数 callback の body に scope、transform 済 module 全体ではない — outer `runBboxPageEvaluate` の keepNames wrap や無関係 wrap ~16 個を除外しないと clean fix が false-RED になる）。非空虚性 2-guard（M-3: named-bound な positive-control fixture は `__name` emit 必須; allowlist-free な Tier2 INV は standing-RED P0 で着地する）。**IO ground-truth**: typecheck 0 / lint 0 / format:check clean / docs:verify 53 PASS / FULL standing 4 ドメイン 138 files・956 tests passed・0 failed; 新 INV file = vitest 26 passed。**実機 DoD**（tsx production-canonical worker による stripe.com の fresh `page.analyze`）: sectionSelector 0→14 + `resolvedCount` 0→18 + `page.evaluate() failed during scroll sweep` warn 消失 — fix が bbox 解決を実際に機能させることを実証。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-07-01。
  - **Per-file change summary / ファイル別変更サマリ**: production: `apps/mcp-server/src/services/part/part-bbox-playwright.service.ts` (`runBboxPageEvaluate` decomposed into argument-position anonymous callbacks; ~110 lines reshaped; combined production diff +75/-55 = net +20, dominated by the in-source comment co-update; feat-only net ≈ -22 per Finding Registry L-7, well under the 800-line hard cap; L-4/L-8/L-9 semantic-boundary + `container` single-assignment + anti-idiom-intent comments) / `packages/config/eslint/index.js` (~20-line scoped `complexity:["error",10]` override comment co-update — the stale `resolveOne / matchInContainer / finalize` references are replaced by the argument-position-callback description, kept in sync with the decomposition, H-1 co-required item 3). test: `apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-page-evaluate-no-name-injection-001.test.ts` (NEW, 246 lines, AGPL-3.0-only SPDX source-direct). / production: `apps/mcp-server/src/services/part/part-bbox-playwright.service.ts`（`runBboxPageEvaluate` を argument-position anonymous callback に分解; ~110 行 reshape; production 合計 diff +75/-55 = net +20、in-source コメント co-update が支配; feat-only net ≈ -22（Finding Registry L-7）、800 行 hard cap を大きく下回る; L-4/L-8/L-9 semantic-boundary + `container` single-assignment + anti-idiom-intent コメント）/ `packages/config/eslint/index.js`（~20 行の scoped `complexity:["error",10]` override コメント co-update — stale な `resolveOne / matchInContainer / finalize` 参照を argument-position-callback 記述に置換、decomposition と同期、H-1 co-required item 3）。test: `apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-page-evaluate-no-name-injection-001.test.ts`（NEW、246 行、AGPL-3.0-only SPDX source 直書き）。

- **Crop persistence — GDPR Art.17 cascade + deep-crop C5 + default flip (package-level; OSS-synced production source) — working-tree (branch `feature/webui`; WebUI v1 W6 Issue A PR-3b; IO Plan Decision V0 = CONDITIONAL `019ef9b2-3c92` / IO Impl Decision V0 = CONDITIONAL `019ef9dc-0a60`; 2026-06-24; T+1d 2026-06-25)** / **Crop 永続化 — GDPR Art.17 cascade + deep-crop C5 + default flip (パッケージレベル; OSS-synced production source)**:
  - **EN — Added**: Wired the crop-dir GDPR Art.17 deletion cascade, closed the deep-crop (C5) coverage gap, and flipped `CROP_PERSISTENCE_ENABLED` default OFF→ON for section/part viewable crops. The `data.delete` cascade now bulk-unlinks `<root>/crops/<webPageId>/` on both the single and batch paths via `deleteCropDirBestEffort`, path-traversal-safe through `buildSafeCropDir` (internal `UUID_REGEX` → the shared `buildSafePathWithinRoot` core), idempotent (`fs.rm force:true recursive`), best-effort (`logger.warn` + `truncateId` + `sanitizeErrorMessage`, no DB rollback). The `processDynamicFallbackBatch` dynamic-fallback path now persists the viewable PNG crop via the leaf helper `persistDynamicFallbackSectionCrop`, co-located with the `vision_embedding` write; PII fail-closed is inherited from the upstream high-PII section filter (no redundant gate; GDPR Art.5(1)(c), not Art.9). Retention unchanged (crops kept until `data.delete`, no TTL cron).
  - **JP — 追加**: section/part viewable crop の crop-dir GDPR Art.17 削除 cascade を配線し、deep-crop (C5) coverage GAP を解消し、`CROP_PERSISTENCE_ENABLED` default を OFF→ON に flip。`data.delete` cascade が single + batch 両 path で `deleteCropDirBestEffort` 経由 `<root>/crops/<webPageId>/` を一括 unlink、path-traversal は `buildSafeCropDir`（内部 `UUID_REGEX` → 共有 `buildSafePathWithinRoot` core）経由、冪等（`fs.rm force:true recursive`）、best-effort（`logger.warn` + `truncateId` + `sanitizeErrorMessage`、DB 削除を巻き戻さない）。`processDynamicFallbackBatch` dynamic-fallback 経路が leaf helper `persistDynamicFallbackSectionCrop` 経由 viewable PNG crop を `vision_embedding` write と co-located 永続化; PII fail-closed は upstream の high-PII section filter を継承（冗長 gate なし; GDPR Art.5(1)(c)、Art.9 ではない）。保持契約は不変（crop は `data.delete` まで保持、TTL cron なし）。

### Fixed / 修正

- **`SERVER_CONFIG.version` stale-drift fix (`0.3.0` → `0.6.0`) + version-coherence test (package-level; OSS-synced production source; MCP handshake `serverInfo.version` correction, no tool I/O change) — working-tree (branch `fix/public-artifact-dogfood-findings`; clean-environment public-artifact dogfood finding; IO Impl Decision = CONDITIONAL `019f5043-2873-7514-a5b8-2a64e48dbbb0` → docs-only unblock; 2026-07-11; T+1d 2026-07-12)** / **`SERVER_CONFIG.version` の stale drift 修正（`0.3.0` → `0.6.0`）+ version coherence テスト（パッケージレベル; OSS-synced production source; MCP handshake `serverInfo.version` 訂正、tool I/O 変更なし）**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: `SERVER_CONFIG.version` in `src/server.ts` — the literal returned as the MCP handshake `serverInfo.version` (`createServer()` → `new Server({ name, version })`) — was a stale `0.3.0` while `apps/mcp-server/package.json.version` was already `0.6.0`. Corrected the literal `0.3.0` → `0.6.0` and added a JSDoc block on `SERVER_CONFIG` documenting the release-time sync requirement and why SSOT-derive (static JSON import / runtime read) is intentionally NOT used (`package.json` is outside `rootDir: ./src`, so a static import breaks the tsc build contract; a runtime read adds `__dirname`/startup-I/O fragility).
  - **JP — 修正**: `src/server.ts` の `SERVER_CONFIG.version` — MCP handshake の `serverInfo.version`（`createServer()` → `new Server({ name, version })`）として返却されるリテラル — が stale な `0.3.0` で、`apps/mcp-server/package.json.version` は既に `0.6.0` だった。リテラル `0.3.0` → `0.6.0` に訂正し、`SERVER_CONFIG` に JSDoc block を追加してリリース時の同期要件と、SSOT-derive（静的 JSON import / 実行時読込）を意図的に不採用とする理由（`package.json` は `rootDir: ./src` 外ゆえ静的 import は tsc build 契約を壊す; 実行時読込は `__dirname`/起動 I/O fragility を生む）を明記。
  - **EN — Tests**: Added `tests/server-version-coherence.test.ts` (NEW, 43 lines, AGPL-3.0-only SPDX) — a plain vitest unit test (no INV-\* number; lightweight-path) asserting `SERVER_CONFIG.version === apps/mcp-server/package.json.version` on every default `pnpm test` / CI run. It reads `package.json` via `readFileSync` + `path.resolve(__dirname, "../package.json")`, asserts `typeof pkg.version === "string"`, then equality. Non-vacuous: a TDA mutation `0.6.0 → 0.3.0` REDs it (byte-exact restore verified).
  - **JP — テスト**: `tests/server-version-coherence.test.ts`（NEW、43 行、AGPL-3.0-only SPDX）を追加 — plain vitest unit test（INV-\* 番号なし; lightweight-path）で `SERVER_CONFIG.version === apps/mcp-server/package.json.version` を毎回の default `pnpm test` / CI 実行で検証。`readFileSync` + `path.resolve(__dirname, "../package.json")` で `package.json` を読み、`typeof pkg.version === "string"` を assert 後に equality を検証。非空虚: TDA mutation `0.6.0 → 0.3.0` で RED（byte-exact 復元で確認）。
  - **Per-file LoC delta / ファイル別 LoC delta**: `src/server.ts` +21/−1 — `SERVER_CONFIG.version` literal `0.3.0` → `0.6.0` (1 line) + a ~20-line bilingual JSDoc on `SERVER_CONFIG` (release-sync requirement + SSOT-derive-rejection rationale). `tests/server-version-coherence.test.ts` NEW +43 — the coherence unit test. / `src/server.ts` +21/−1 — `SERVER_CONFIG.version` リテラル `0.3.0` → `0.6.0`（1 行）+ `SERVER_CONFIG` への ~20 行 bilingual JSDoc（リリース同期要件 + SSOT-derive 不採用理由）。`tests/server-version-coherence.test.ts` NEW +43 — coherence unit test。
  - **Env var / DB migration / MCP tool / audit action / enum**: none — a version-literal correction + a unit test only (no MCP tool I/O schema surface; `gen:tool-count` unchanged at 40; NOT in INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 scope; no migration; no env var; no audit action). / なし — version リテラル訂正 + unit test のみ（MCP tool I/O schema に非 surface; `gen:tool-count` は 40 のまま; INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 scope 外; migration なし; env var なし; audit action なし）。

- **Redis client `error` listener attached in all environments — resolve a CI false-red where an all-green unit run exits 1 (package-level; OSS-synced production source; production observability improvement, no MCP tool I/O change) — working-tree (branch `feature/webui`; LCC CONDITIONAL unblock, Option A; 2026-07-06)** / **Redis クライアントの `error` リスナーを全環境で attach — 全 green の unit run が exit 1 になる CI false-red を解消（パッケージレベル; OSS-synced production source; production 観測性の改善、MCP tool I/O 変更なし）**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Moved the ioredis `error` listener in `src/config/redis.ts` out of the `NODE_ENV === "development"`-only guard so it is attached in every environment (routed through `logger.warn` — silent in the test environment unless `ENABLE_TEST_LOGS=true`, a warning in dev/prod — and logging only `err.message`, so no connection URL or credentials are exposed, CWE-209 clean). Without a listener, ioredis falls back to `console.error("[ioredis] Unhandled error event", ...)` whenever Redis is unreachable; in CI unit jobs (which run without a Redis service) that background console output races vitest's RPC teardown and surfaces as a spurious teardown error that fails an otherwise all-passing run with exit 1. Attaching the listener unconditionally removes that race structurally, and — since production previously had no `error` listener at all — also improves Redis connection-error observability. This corrects the ` §isDevelopment() guard principle, which prohibits gating error-handling logging behind a development-only guard.
  - **JP — 修正**: `src/config/redis.ts` の ioredis `error` リスナーを `NODE_ENV === "development"` 限定ガードの外に出し、全環境で attach するようにした（`logger.warn` 経由 — test 環境では `ENABLE_TEST_LOGS=true` の場合を除き silent、dev/prod では warning — かつ `err.message` のみを出力するため接続 URL / 認証情報は非露出、CWE-209 clean）。リスナー不在時、ioredis は Redis 到達不能時に `console.error("[ioredis] Unhandled error event", ...)` へ fallback し、CI の unit ジョブ（Redis サービスなしで実行）ではその background console 出力が vitest の RPC teardown と競合して spurious な teardown error を発生させ、全テスト passed でも exit 1 になる（false-red）。リスナーを無条件に attach することでこの race を構造的に解消し、かつ production は従来 `error` リスナーが皆無だったため Redis 接続エラーの観測性も改善する。これは error-handling ログを development 限定ガードで抑制することを禁じる ` §isDevelopment() ガード原則への是正である。
  - **Per-file LoC delta / ファイル別 LoC delta**: `src/config/redis.ts` +13/−5 — added the unconditional `error` listener (`client.on("error", …)` → `logger.warn`, `err.message` only) + an 8-line explanatory comment; removed the old `NODE_ENV === "development"`-guarded `console.error` `error` listener + its stale comment. / `src/config/redis.ts` +13/−5 — 無条件 `error` リスナー（`client.on("error", …)` → `logger.warn`、`err.message` のみ）+ 8 行の説明コメントを追加; 旧 `NODE_ENV === "development"` 限定 `console.error` `error` リスナー + stale コメントを削除。
  - **Verification / 検証**: dead-port run: unhandled `[ioredis]` console output 106 → 0 lines / standing regression suite 138 files GREEN / full unit suite 519 files, 16,259 tests, 0 failed. / dead-port 実行: unhandled `[ioredis]` console 出力 106 → 0 行 / standing regression suite 138 files GREEN / full unit suite 519 files, 16,259 tests, 0 failed。
  - **Env var / DB migration / MCP tool / audit action / enum**: none — Redis client error-listener placement only (`gen:tool-count` unchanged at 40; not in INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 scope). / なし — Redis クライアントの error-listener 配置のみ（`gen:tool-count` は 40 のまま; INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 scope 外）。
  - **Refs / 参照**: `§isDevelopment() guard principle (correction) /`src/config/redis.ts` (`createRedisClient`error listener) / root`CHANGELOG.md` `[Unreleased]`Fixed (OSS-facing) /` `[Unreleased]` Fixed (internal full).

### Security / セキュリティ

- **CodeQL hardening — `truncateOrigJobId` `js/incorrect-suffix-check` resolution (package-level; OSS-synced production source; 1-char validation hardening, no runtime behavior change) — working-tree (branch `fix/codeql-suffix-check`; IO Impl Decision = APPROVE `019e913e`; 2026-06-04; T+1d 2026-06-05)** / **CodeQL ハードニング — `truncateOrigJobId` の `js/incorrect-suffix-check` 解消 (パッケージレベル; OSS-synced production source; 1 文字の validation hardening、runtime 挙動変更なし)**:
  - **EN — Security**: Resolved CodeQL alert `js/incorrect-suffix-check` in `truncateOrigJobId` (`src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts`) by changing the `<webPageId>__<category>` separator-position guard from `sepIndex === origJobId.length - 2` to `sepIndex >= origJobId.length - 2` (a length-based comparison CodeQL accepts) and adding a 5-line explanatory comment. **No production runtime behavior change (validation hardening, CodeQL `js/incorrect-suffix-check` resolution only; `>=` is equivalent to `===` since `indexOf("__")` ≤ length-2 for a found separator)** — `indexOf("__")` can return at most `length - 2` for a found separator, so `>=` matches exactly the same inputs as `===`. `vitest` / `@vitest/coverage-v8` and the published runtime dependency tree / SBOM are unaffected.
  - **JP — セキュリティ**: `truncateOrigJobId` (`src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts`) の CodeQL alert `js/incorrect-suffix-check` を、`<webPageId>__<category>` separator 位置の guard を `sepIndex === origJobId.length - 2` から `sepIndex >= origJobId.length - 2` (CodeQL が受理する length-based 比較) へ変更し、5 行の説明コメントを追加して解消。**production runtime の挙動変更なし (validation hardening、CodeQL `js/incorrect-suffix-check` の解消のみ; found separator では `indexOf("__")` ≤ length-2 ゆえ `>=` は `===` と等価)** — found separator の `indexOf("__")` は最大でも `length - 2` であり、`>=` は `===` と完全に同一の入力に match する。`vitest` / `@vitest/coverage-v8` および公開 runtime 依存ツリー / SBOM は非影響。
  - **Per-file LoC delta / ファイル別 LoC delta**: `src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts` +6/−1 (the `===` → `>=` operator 1-char change on the guard line + a 5-line explanatory comment; logic delta is a single character) / guard 行の `===` → `>=` 1 文字変更 + 5 行説明コメント; logic delta は 1 文字。
  - **Env var / DB migration / MCP tool / audit action**: none — validation-guard hardening only / なし — validation-guard ハードニングのみ。

### Tests / テスト

- **CO-DID bundle — URL-uniqueness real-DB integration test + URL-normalize SSOT src-wide AST sweep (package-level; test-only; 3 files; no production source/dist change) — working-tree (branch `chore/co-did-bundle`; IO Plan Decision V1 = APPROVE `019e9421` / IO Impl Decision V1 = APPROVE `019e943b`; 2026-06-05; T+1d 2026-06-06)** / **CO-DID bundle — URL 一意性 real-DB integration test + URL 正規化 SSOT src-wide AST sweep (パッケージレベル; test-only; 3 ファイル; production source/dist 変更なし)**:
  - **EN — Tests**: Test-only defense-in-depth bundle landing CO-DID-01/02/03 (no production source/dist change, no DB migration, no env var, no MCP tool, no audit action). **(CO-DID-01)** New real-DB integration test `tests/integration/web-page-url-unique.integration.test.ts` (`INV-WEBPAGE-URL-UNIQUE-DB-002`, **3 tests**) verifies the `web_pages.url` UNIQUE constraint (`web_pages_url_key`) end-to-end against a live PostgreSQL: a CI environment precondition (CI MUST provide `DATABASE_URL`, local degrades to a documented precondition-assert — NOT `.skip`), `findOrCreateByUrl` find-first idempotency (one row, same id), and a same-url raw `create` rejected with Prisma `P2002`. Per-test `uuidv4` url + `afterEach` cleanup. **(CO-DID-02)** `tests/utils/inv-url-normalize-ssot-001.test.ts` `INV-URL-NORMALIZE-SSOT-001` DB-upsert sweep upgraded from a text-regex 4-file `KNOWN_DB_UPSERT_FILES` sanity-list (now **removed**) to a src-wide AST CallExpression discovery (`discoverWebPageUrlUpsertCallsites`, walking `prisma.webPage.{create,upsert,update}` calls with a `url` in `data`). The AST sweep discovered **7** callsites (forecast 5→7 corrected at IO Impl Decision V1: the old 4-file list under-counted `db-handler.ts` + `worker-supervisor-failure-path.service.ts`), 6 routing through `normalizeUrlForStorage` + 1 documented exception. **(CO-DID-03)** Option B (production unchanged): `web-page.service.ts:207` raw create registered in a `DOCUMENTED_RAWCREATE_EXCEPTIONS` allowlist (unregistered bypass FAILs the sweep). CWE-697 latent dedup degradation is documented accepted-risk (real-harm 0 via data-layer dual defense: DB `web_pages_url_key` UNIQUE + `findOrCreateByUrl` find-first idempotency). **(Hotfix, same-day)** The CO-DID-01 P2002 assertion was changed from `toBeInstanceOf(Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError)` to a module-resolution-robust duck-type (`.name` + `.code`); `instanceof` is brittle across the OSS bundle module boundaries and RED-ed the OSS CI `test:integration` job while the local FULL run short-circuited without `DATABASE_URL`. Re-verified against the live local DB (`DATABASE_URL` set, real P2002 path, 3/3 pass).
  - **JP — テスト**: CO-DID-01/02/03 を着地させる test-only defense-in-depth bundle (production source/dist 変更なし、DB migration なし、env var なし、MCP tool なし、audit action なし)。**(CO-DID-01)** 新規 real-DB integration test `tests/integration/web-page-url-unique.integration.test.ts` (`INV-WEBPAGE-URL-UNIQUE-DB-002`、**3 tests**) が `web_pages.url` UNIQUE 制約 (`web_pages_url_key`) を稼働中の PostgreSQL に対し end-to-end に検証: CI 環境 precondition (CI は `DATABASE_URL` 提供必須、local は documented precondition-assert に degrade — `.skip` ではない)、`findOrCreateByUrl` find-first idempotency (1 行、同一 id)、同一 url の raw `create` が Prisma `P2002` で reject。per-test `uuidv4` url + `afterEach` cleanup。**(CO-DID-02)** `tests/utils/inv-url-normalize-ssot-001.test.ts` の `INV-URL-NORMALIZE-SSOT-001` DB-upsert sweep を、text-regex な 4-file `KNOWN_DB_UPSERT_FILES` sanity-list (現在 **削除**) から src-wide AST CallExpression discovery (`discoverWebPageUrlUpsertCallsites`、`data` に `url` を持つ `prisma.webPage.{create,upsert,update}` call を走査) に強化。AST sweep は **7** callsite discover (IO Impl Decision V1 で forecast 5→7 訂正: 旧 4-file list が `db-handler.ts` + `worker-supervisor-failure-path.service.ts` を under-count)、6 件 `normalizeUrlForStorage` 経由 + 1 件 documented exception。**(CO-DID-03)** Option B (production 無改変): `web-page.service.ts:207` raw create を `DOCUMENTED_RAWCREATE_EXCEPTIONS` allowlist に登録 (未登録 bypass は sweep FAIL)。CWE-697 latent dedup degradation は documented accepted-risk (real-harm 0、data-layer 2 重防御: DB `web_pages_url_key` UNIQUE + `findOrCreateByUrl` find-first idempotency)。
  - **Test files / テストファイル (per-file LoC delta + test counts)**:

### Added / 追加

- **WebUI v1 W5 honest-rendering F1-F4 — internal read-API honest display contract: similar `rank` + featured quality-first seed + MMR diversity + moodAvgConfidence null-honesty + grade D zero-fill — package-level; ADR-0042 Amendment 10 — branch `feature/webui`; 2026-06-22** / **WebUI v1 W5 honest-rendering F1-F4 — 内部 read API の honest 表示契約: 類似 `rank` + featured quality-first seed + MMR 多様化 + moodAvgConfidence null-honesty + grade D zero-fill — パッケージレベル; ADR-0042 Amendment 10**:
  - **EN — Changed / Added (package-level)**: Modified 2 + added 1 file under `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/` to render honest quality / similarity / mood (no new ML / scorer redesign). **`page-detail.service.ts` (F2)**: `SimilarDesign` gains `rank: number`, **assigned on the POST-filter array** in `getSimilarDesignsWithMood` (`.filter(self).map((row, index) => ({ ..., rank: index + 1 }))` — no rank gap from the self-filter; distance-ASC rows → honest nearest ordinal); the raw cosine `similarity` is retained on the wire for back-compat (NOT UI-load-bearing); `mood_category` stays adapter-internal (`getSimilarDesigns` strips it from the public shape, the F4 MMR proxy only). The `SimilarDesign.rank` JSDoc documents the **dual path-relative semantics** (page-detail = distance rank / featured = MMR display order overwrite). **`featured-diversity.ts` (NEW, F4)**: `mmrSelect(pool, limit, lambda)` + the extracted `pickNextDiversified(remaining, selected, lambda)` (greedy MMR, `score = λ * relevance - (1-λ) * maxProxySimToSelected`, `MMR_LAMBDA = 0.7`, relevance-dominant), `proxySimilarity` = an embedding-free mood/host proxy (0.5 + 0.5), `OVERFETCH_FACTOR = 3`, `MAX_FEATURED_OVERFETCH = MAX_SIMILAR_LIMIT * OVERFETCH_FACTOR` (=36, SSOT-derived — no `*_HARD` ghost / no `NaN` LIMIT, CWE-770). Loads NO embeddings into JS / imports NO `@reftrixmcp/ml`/Prisma (`INV-WEBUI-READONLY-NEGATIVE-001` layer boundary). CC ≤10 via the `pickNextDiversified` extraction (`src/api/internal/**` machine-enforced `complexity:["error",10]`). SPDX `AGPL-3.0-only` header. **`dashboard.service.ts` (F4 + F1/F3)**: `getFeaturedComparison` now uses a quality-first 4-key seed `ORDER BY COALESCE(qe.overall_score,-1) DESC, (screenshot present) DESC, <real-domain> DESC, wp.id ASC` (latest score via the existing `LEFT JOIN LATERAL ... ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`; explicit-seed WHERE → ORDER BY no-op) + over-fetch + `mmrSelect` re-rank (1..N display order overwrite) + `moodCategory` strip; `buildQualityGradeDistribution` zero-fills the **5 grades `{A,B,C,D,F}`** (scorer `scoreToGrade` SSOT, the prior A/B/C/F-only undercounted a D page) and `QualityGradeDistribution` is the 5-key type; `resolveMoodAvgConfidence` resolves `DashboardStats.moodAvgConfidence: number|null` (null-honesty: `null` on zero narratives, non-finite → `null`, clamped [0,1]). **`featured-comparison` is an HTTP route, NOT a new MCP tool** (`gen:tool-count` = 39 unchanged). **No migration, no enum, no worker, no GDPR-delete path** (`Regression domain: none`). **M6 EXPLAIN ANALYZE acceptance**: the F4 seed correlated LATERAL is sub-3ms (live 2.078ms) on the existing 2-col `quality_evaluations_target_type_target_id_idx` — the composite `(target_type,target_id,created_at)` index is ABSENT (verified via `pg_indexes`), composite index deferred to a future migration (recorded in ADR-0042 Amendment 10 §A10.5). Carryover (L, all accepted-risk / docs / defense-in-depth, deadline 2026-06-23): #5 (SEC) `buildRealDomainPreferenceSql(urlColumn)` injectable IF user input passed, but the only caller is the fixed literal `"wp.url"` (L5 hard-assert pins no-interpolation; defense-in-depth: narrow `urlColumn` to an internal enum, owner backend-api-developer); #6 (TDA) `bandLabelKey` helper duplicated across webui featured / similar components (prefix differs, owner frontend-engineer). Documented in ADR-0042 **Amendment 10** (§A10.1–A10.8). AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-22. Modified OSS-synced production files: `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/dashboard.service.ts`, `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/page-detail.service.ts`, `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/featured-diversity.ts` (new).
  - **JP — 変更 / 追加 (パッケージレベル)**: `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/` 配下を 2 変更 + 1 新規し、品質 / 類似度 / mood を honest に描く (新規 ML / scorer redesign なし)。**`page-detail.service.ts` (F2)**: `SimilarDesign` に `rank: number` を追加、`getSimilarDesignsWithMood` 内で **post-filter array 上で assign** (`.filter(self).map((row, index) => ({ ..., rank: index + 1 }))` — self-filter で rank gap なし; distance-ASC rows → honest nearest ordinal); 生 cosine `similarity` は back-compat で wire 上に retain (UI load-bearing ではない); `mood_category` は adapter-internal に留まる (`getSimilarDesigns` が public shape から strip、F4 MMR proxy 専用)。`SimilarDesign.rank` JSDoc が **dual path-relative semantics** を記述 (page-detail = distance rank / featured = MMR 表示順 overwrite)。**`featured-diversity.ts` (新規、F4)**: `mmrSelect(pool, limit, lambda)` + 抽出した `pickNextDiversified(remaining, selected, lambda)` (greedy MMR、`score = λ * relevance - (1-λ) * maxProxySimToSelected`、`MMR_LAMBDA = 0.7`、relevance-dominant)、`proxySimilarity` = embedding-free mood/host proxy (0.5 + 0.5)、`OVERFETCH_FACTOR = 3`、`MAX_FEATURED_OVERFETCH = MAX_SIMILAR_LIMIT * OVERFETCH_FACTOR` (=36、SSOT-derived — `*_HARD` 捏造なし / `NaN` LIMIT なし、CWE-770)。embedding を JS にロードしない / `@reftrixmcp/ml`/Prisma 非 import (`INV-WEBUI-READONLY-NEGATIVE-001` 層境界)。`pickNextDiversified` 抽出で CC ≤10 (`src/api/internal/**` machine-enforced `complexity:["error",10]`)。SPDX `AGPL-3.0-only` header。**`dashboard.service.ts` (F4 + F1/F3)**: `getFeaturedComparison` は quality-first 4-key seed `ORDER BY COALESCE(qe.overall_score,-1) DESC, (screenshot present) DESC, <real-domain> DESC, wp.id ASC` を使用 (最新 score を既存 `LEFT JOIN LATERAL ... ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1` で読む; 明示 seed の WHERE → ORDER BY no-op) + over-fetch + `mmrSelect` re-rank (1..N 表示順 overwrite) + `moodCategory` strip; `buildQualityGradeDistribution` は **5 grade `{A,B,C,D,F}`** を zero-fill (scorer `scoreToGrade` SSOT、従前の A/B/C/F-only は D ページを undercount) し `QualityGradeDistribution` は 5-key 型; `resolveMoodAvgConfidence` が `DashboardStats.moodAvgConfidence: number|null` を解決 (null-honesty: narrative 皆無で `null`、non-finite → `null`、[0,1] clamp)。**`featured-comparison` は HTTP route であり新規 MCP tool ではない** (`gen:tool-count` = 39 不変)。**migration なし・enum なし・worker なし・GDPR 削除経路なし** (`Regression domain: none`)。**M6 EXPLAIN ANALYZE acceptance**: F4 seed correlated LATERAL は既存 2-col `quality_evaluations_target_type_target_id_idx` で sub-3ms (live 2.078ms) — composite `(target_type,target_id,created_at)` index は不在 (`pg_indexes` 検証済)、composite index は将来 migration へ defer (ADR-0042 Amendment 10 §A10.5 に記録)。carryover (L、全 accepted-risk / docs / defense-in-depth、deadline 2026-06-23): #5 (SEC) `buildRealDomainPreferenceSql(urlColumn)` は user input を渡せば injectable だが唯一の caller は固定リテラル `"wp.url"` (L5 hard-assert が no-interpolation を pin; defense-in-depth: `urlColumn` を internal enum に narrow、owner backend-api-developer); #6 (TDA) `bandLabelKey` helper が webui featured / similar component 間で重複 (prefix が異なる、owner frontend-engineer)。ADR-0042 **Amendment 10** (§A10.1–A10.8) に記録。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-06-22。改変 OSS-synced production ファイル: `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/dashboard.service.ts`、`apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/page-detail.service.ts`、`apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/featured-diversity.ts` (新規)。

- **WebUI v1 W5 — internal read-API dashboard intelligence: featured-comparison route + additive aggregate fields — package-level; ADR-0042 Amendment 9 — branch `feature/webui`; 2026-06-21** / **WebUI v1 W5 — 内部 read API ダッシュボード知性化: featured-comparison route + additive aggregate フィールド — パッケージレベル; ADR-0042 Amendment 9**:
  - **EN — Added (package-level)**: Added 1 read-only route + additive aggregate fields to `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/`. **New route `GET /internal/dashboard/featured-comparison`** (`getFeaturedComparison` in `dashboard.service.ts`, `handleFeaturedComparison` + `app.get(...)` in `server.ts`, `featuredComparisonQuerySchema` in `schemas.ts`): a deterministic seed page + its top-N pgvector neighbors by **reusing the existing `getSimilarDesigns`** (zero-ML self-referential subquery, ADR-0042 Decision 3 DRY) — **no live embedding inference** (DB pre-computed pgvector KNN; `@reftrixmcp/ml` not booted). `seed` `z.string().regex(UUID_REGEX).optional()` (canonical `UUID_REGEX` SSOT) + `limit` 1..12 default 6 (`MAX_SIMILAR_LIMIT` / `DEFAULT_SIMILAR_LIMIT`, CWE-770). Honest empty `{seed: null, similar: []}` when no narrative embedding exists; an explicit-seed-without-embedding is echoed with `similar: []`. **Seed 3-key soft preference (auto-pick)**: `ORDER BY <real-domain> DESC, (screenshot present) DESC, wp.id ASC LIMIT 1` (RFC 2606 reserved domains de-prioritized first, then screenshot-present, then smallest id) — soft preferences, NOT hard filters (the `EXISTS (... embedding IS NOT NULL ...)` guard fixes the candidate set); the ENTIRE ORDER BY is a **no-op for an explicit seed** (WHERE narrows to a single `wp.id = $1::uuid` row). SQL-injection-safe: `buildRealDomainPreferenceSql` assembles the real-domain clause from the `RESERVED_SHOWCASE_URL_PATTERNS` SSOT (parameter-free, non-interpolated); seed `$1::uuid` parameter-bound; pgvector KNN parameter-bound. **Additive aggregate fields** (no breaking change): `DashboardStats.qualityGradeDistribution` `{A,B,C,F}` (always-present zero-filled + honest fold-in of other grades via `buildQualityGradeDistribution`; `quality_evaluations.grade` groupBy) + `DashboardStats.moodDistribution` `[{mood, count}]` (desc, honest `[]`, raw `MoodCategory` enum — INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 no new values); `RecentPageSummary` `qualityScore: number|null` (honest N/A) / `qualityGrade: string|null` / `moodCategory: string|null` + `sectionCount`/`partCount`/`motionCount` (`_count` aggregate, **N+1-free** via `getLatestQualityByPageIds` batch). New SSOT helpers in `dashboard.service.ts`: `RESERVED_SHOWCASE_URL_PATTERNS` + `buildRealDomainPreferenceSql` + `buildQualityGradeDistribution` + `getLatestQualityByPageIds`. The aggregate fields **only extend the existing `getDashboardStats` / `getRecentPages` routes** (the only new route is featured-comparison). **`featured-comparison` is an HTTP route, NOT a new MCP tool registration** (`gen:tool-count` = 39 unchanged). **No migration, no enum, no worker, no GDPR-delete path** (`Regression domain: none`). TDA Health 96/100, all functions CC ≤4 (measured). Carryover (L, all accepted-risk / defense-in-depth, deadline 2026-06-22): L-5 (F-TDA-02) — `dashboard.service.ts` outside the eslint scoped complexity override (CC ≤4 but not machine-enforced; defense-in-depth: add `src/api/internal/**/*.ts` to the `packages/config/eslint/index.js` scoped override, owner backend-api-developer). Documented in ADR-0042 **Amendment 9** (§A9.1–A9.5). AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-21. - **WebUI v1 W4a — internal read-API compare route (`POST /internal/compare`) + `internal_compare` rate-limiter tier — package-level; ADR-0042 Amendment 7 — branch `feature/webui`; 2026-06-20** / **WebUI v1 W4a — 内部 read API compare route (`POST /internal/compare`) + `internal_compare` rate-limiter tier — パッケージレベル; ADR-0042 Amendment 7**:
  - **EN — Added (package-level)**: Added a read-only compare route + a rate-limiter tier key to `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/`: `POST /internal/compare` in the new `compare.service.ts` (`getCompare` — a **thin adapter calling the existing `designCompareHandler` directly (案b)**: the handler self-contains the SearchCache + duplicate-page_id reject (`Set`) + `sanitizeErrorMessage`, so `compareDesigns`-direct (案a) is avoided — DRY, ADR-0042 Decision 3 + Amendment 5 handler-direct precedent); `compareBodySchema` + `COMPARE_DIMENSIONS` (pinned `=== ALL_DIMENSIONS` by a drift test) + `MAX_COMPARE_BODY_BYTES = 4096` added to `schemas.ts` (`page_ids` 2..5 from the `UUID_REGEX` export SSOT, `dimensions` 1..4 enum, `include_details`; CWE-770); `handleCompare` + `compareFailureStatus` added to `server.ts` (the route scopes `express.json({ limit })` after the limiter). compare performs **no live embedding inference** (DB pre-computed read) so it does NOT use the ADR-0043 `degradedReason` enum (honest-rendering principle only). **Status mapping**: a Zod reject → `400` fixed string (CWE-209); `success:false` → error-code-prefix branch `PAGES_NOT_FOUND` → 404 / `INVALID_INPUT` → 400 / otherwise → 503; `success:true` → 200; the structured body is always returned (honest fail-loud, never a fake empty matrix). **SEC-rate-limiter wiring (MUST, CWE-770)**: `internal_compare: "search"` added to `TOOL_TIER_MAP` in `middleware/rate-limiter.ts` at the **search tier (120 RPM)**, sharing the same Token Bucket core + same Lua as the MCP-tool path; the route wires `internalRateLimit("internal_compare")` **BEFORE** `express.json` (over-limit → 429 before body parse). HTTP-route adapter = **fail-open** on a limiter throw (127.0.0.1-fixed, opt-in default-OFF, read-only dogfood); MCP-tool path (`router.ts`) = **fail-closed** (intentional divergence). **`internal_compare` is an HTTP route key, NOT a new MCP tool registration** — the MCP tool count is unchanged (`gen:tool-count` = 39); it appears only in the rate-limit `TOOL_TIER_MAP` + the rate-limit tier docs (with a "not MCP tools" distinction note). **No migration, no enum, no worker, no GDPR-delete path** (`Regression domain: none`). The internal read-API process does not boot `@reftrixmcp/ml` (ADR-0042 layer boundary). Documented in ADR-0042 **Amendment 7** (§A7.1–A7.8). - **WebUI v1 W2 v2 — internal read endpoints for the human-meaningful page-detail viewer (`getPageNarrative` / `getSimilarDesigns` + `getPageQuality` recommendations; similar = zero-ML self-referential subquery) — package-level; ADR-0042 Amendment 4 — branch `feature/webui`; 2026-06-19** / **WebUI v1 W2 v2 — 人間に意味のある page-detail viewer 向け内部 read endpoint (`getPageNarrative` / `getSimilarDesigns` + `getPageQuality` recommendations; similar = zero-ML self-referential subquery) — パッケージレベル; ADR-0042 Amendment 4**:
  - **EN — Added (package-level)**: Added 2 read-only internal-API endpoints + 1 field to `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/`: `getPageNarrative` (`GET /internal/pages/:webPageId/narrative`, null = "unanalyzed") and `getSimilarDesigns` (`GET /internal/pages/:webPageId/similar?limit=N`) in `page-detail.service.ts`, plus `recommendations: string[]` added to `getPageQuality`; 2 routes added to `server.ts`; `similarQuerySchema` (`limit` 1..12 default 6, CWE-770) added to `schemas.ts`. Inherits ADR-0042 Decisions 1/3/4/5/6/7 (under `/internal` through `internalAuthSeam`, Zod boundary, fixed-string 404, `sanitizeErrorMessage`). **`getSimilarDesigns` = the read-only service's first `$queryRawUnsafe`** — a zero-ML single-SQL self-referential subquery (NEW pattern, no codebase precedent): `dne.embedding <=> (SELECT dne2.embedding FROM design_narrative_embeddings dne2 JOIN design_narratives dn2 ON dn2.id = dne2.design_narrative_id WHERE dn2.web_page_id = $1::uuid AND dne2.embedding IS NOT NULL)`, with no JS round-trip (`::text`/`parseVectorString`/`vectorString` not used), source-NULL graceful (subquery NULL → 0 rows = honest empty), self-exclusion (`dn.web_page_id != $1::uuid` + a service-layer `.filter`), `$1/$2` param-bind only (injection surface 0), and `similarity = 1 - distance` clamped via `Number.isFinite(x) ? clamp(x) : 0`. Source-pinned by `INV-WEBUI-SIMILAR-READONLY-SELECT-001` (0 write-verbs + 0 stacked-`;` + self-exclusion clause + 0 `@reftrixmcp/ml` import + 0 service-wide write-verbs, `DELETE`-injection mutation-proof) + `INV-WEBUI-SIMILAR-SELF-EXCLUSION-001`. The internal read-API process does not boot `@reftrixmcp/ml` (ADR-0042 layer boundary). The narrative JSON columns are not selected (GDPR Art.5(1)(c)). The `/sections` `/parts` routes + service methods are retained (`server.test.ts` route tests kept; webui non-consumer only). **No migration, no enum, no worker, no GDPR-delete path** (`Regression domain: none`). Documented in ADR-0042 **Amendment 4** (§A4.1/§A4.3/§A4.4). AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-19. Modified OSS-synced production files: `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/page-detail.service.ts`, `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/server.ts`, `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/schemas.ts`.
  - **JP — 追加 (パッケージレベル)**: `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/` に read-only endpoint 2 本 + field 1 個を追加: `page-detail.service.ts` に `getPageNarrative` (`GET /internal/pages/:webPageId/narrative`、null = 「未分析」) + `getSimilarDesigns` (`GET /internal/pages/:webPageId/similar?limit=N`)、`getPageQuality` に `recommendations: string[]` 追加; `server.ts` に route 2 本; `schemas.ts` に `similarQuerySchema` (`limit` 1..12 default 6、CWE-770)。ADR-0042 Decision 1/3/4/5/6/7 継承 (`/internal` 配下 → `internalAuthSeam`、Zod 境界、固定文字列 404、`sanitizeErrorMessage`)。**`getSimilarDesigns` = read-only service 初の `$queryRawUnsafe`** — zero-ML 単一 SQL self-referential subquery (既存 precedent なしの新パターン): `dne.embedding <=> (SELECT dne2.embedding FROM design_narrative_embeddings dne2 JOIN design_narratives dn2 ON dn2.id = dne2.design_narrative_id WHERE dn2.web_page_id = $1::uuid AND dne2.embedding IS NOT NULL)`、JS round-trip (`::text`/`parseVectorString`/`vectorString`) 排除、source-NULL graceful (subquery NULL → 0 行 = honest 空)、self-exclusion (`dn.web_page_id != $1::uuid` + service-layer `.filter`)、`$1/$2` param-bind のみ (injection surface 0)、`similarity = 1 - distance` を `Number.isFinite(x) ? clamp(x) : 0` で clamp。`INV-WEBUI-SIMILAR-READONLY-SELECT-001` (write-verb 0 + stacked-`;` 0 + self-exclusion 句 + `@reftrixmcp/ml` import 0 + service 全体 write-verb 0、`DELETE` 注入 mutation-proof) + `INV-WEBUI-SIMILAR-SELF-EXCLUSION-001` で source-pin。内部 read-API プロセスは `@reftrixmcp/ml` を boot しない (ADR-0042 層境界)。narrative JSON 列は非 select (GDPR Art.5(1)(c))。`/sections` `/parts` route + service method は残置 (`server.test.ts` route test 維持、webui 非 consumer 化のみ)。**migration なし・enum なし・worker なし・GDPR 削除経路なし** (`Regression domain: none`)。ADR-0042 **Amendment 4** (§A4.1/§A4.3/§A4.4) に記録。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-06-19。改変 OSS-synced production ファイル: `apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/page-detail.service.ts`、`apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/server.ts`、`apps/mcp-server/src/api/internal/schemas.ts`。

- **WebUI v1 W2 rework — internal full-screenshot serve endpoint (`GET /internal/pages/:webPageId/screenshot`) via screenshot-persistence SSOT resolver chain (CWE-22 defense) — package-level; ADR-0042 Amendment 3 — branch `feature/webui`; 2026-06-16** / **WebUI v1 W2 rework — screenshot-persistence SSOT リゾルバチェーン経由の内部フルスクリーンショット配信 endpoint (`GET /internal/pages/:webPageId/screenshot`、CWE-22 防御) — パッケージレベル; ADR-0042 Amendment 3**:
  - **EN — Added**: Added a read-only internal-API endpoint `GET /internal/pages/:webPageId/screenshot` (`127.0.0.1:24006`, `internalAuthSeam`-gated) serving the **full persisted screenshot** as `image/png` + `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff` + `Content-Length`, with a **route-local 404 (status-only, no JSON body)** registered separate from the shared `asyncRoute`/500-JSON `errorMiddleware`, + 400 on a bad UUID. **CWE-22 path-traversal defense (resolver chain)**: `page-detail.service.getScreenshotStream` resolves via the screenshot-persistence SSOT `getScreenshotPath(webPageId)` (DB-first + `path.resolve` + `startsWith(phase5Dir)` allowlist + `fs.access`) **then** `validateScreenshotPath` (the realpath + `isFile` SSOT) before `fs.createReadStream(safePath)` — 0 raw `screenshot_storage_path`→fs callsites; a `<phase5Dir>/<uuid>.png` symlink escaping the allowlist → null → 404 (IO-mutation-proven non-vacuous). **NO Sharp/crop/clamp code** — per-section image crops were descoped (decision `019ecfe4`, coordinate-space mismatch; the image sink is screenshot-serve-only). `UUID_REGEX` is SSOT-exported from `screenshot-persistence.service.ts` (reused by the route + repair script). Screenshot retention unchanged (until `data.delete`, ADR-0041; `getScreenshotPath` null → 404 post-deletion; `INV-SCREENSHOT-RETENTION-001` unchanged). `vitest` / `@vitest/coverage-v8` / the published runtime dependency tree / SBOM are unaffected. No new runtime dependency.
  - **JP — 追加**: read-only 内部 API endpoint `GET /internal/pages/:webPageId/screenshot` (`127.0.0.1:24006`、`internalAuthSeam` gated) を追加し、**永続済フルスクリーンショット**を `image/png` + `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff` + `Content-Length` で配信、shared `asyncRoute`/500-JSON `errorMiddleware` とは別登録の **route-local 404 (status-only、JSON body なし)** + bad UUID で 400。**CWE-22 path-traversal 防御 (resolver chain)**: `page-detail.service.getScreenshotStream` は screenshot-persistence SSOT `getScreenshotPath(webPageId)` (DB-first + `path.resolve` + `startsWith(phase5Dir)` allowlist + `fs.access`) → **`validateScreenshotPath` (realpath + `isFile` SSOT)** → `fs.createReadStream(safePath)` の順で解決 (raw `screenshot_storage_path`→fs callsite 0); allowlist 外を指す `<phase5Dir>/<uuid>.png` symlink → null → 404 (IO-mutation-proven non-vacuous)。**Sharp/crop/clamp コードなし** — セクション別 image crop は descope (decision `019ecfe4`、座標空間不整合; image sink は screenshot-serve のみ)。`UUID_REGEX` は `screenshot-persistence.service.ts` から SSOT export (route + repair script が reuse)。screenshot 保持不変 (`data.delete` まで、ADR-0041; `getScreenshotPath` は削除後 null → 404; `INV-SCREENSHOT-RETENTION-001` 不変)。`vitest` / `@vitest/coverage-v8` / 公開 runtime 依存ツリー / SBOM は非影響。新規 runtime 依存なし。
  - **Per-file changes / ファイル別変更**: `src/api/internal/page-detail.service.ts` (`getScreenshotStream` resolver chain → read-only stream) / `src/api/internal/server.ts` (route-local screenshot route, status-only 404/400, registered separate from `asyncRoute`) / `src/api/internal/schemas.ts` (reuse `webPageIdParamSchema` / `UUID_REGEX` SSOT, no 2nd regex, CC≤10 no eslint-disable) / `src/services/screenshot-persistence.service.ts` (`UUID_REGEX` SSOT export; `getScreenshotPath` DB-first+startsWith+fs.access vs `validateScreenshotPath` +realpath+isFile capability split). New tests: `tests/api/internal/screenshot-route.test.ts` (7 traversal fixtures + real symlink→404 fixture + 404-no-JSON-body + content-type/nosniff/Content-Length + AST read-only WRITE_VERB_RE=[]).
  - **Env var / DB migration / MCP tool / audit action**: none — internal-API read surface only; no INV among the 4 standing-regression domains (`INV-WEBUI-SCREENSHOT-001..008` are webui-readonly negative-confirm domain; gdpr-delete `INV-SCREENSHOT-RETENTION-001` negative-confirm unchanged) / なし — 内部 API read surface のみ; 常設 regression 4 ドメイン非該当。

- **Embedding cache env vars (`EMBEDDING_CACHE_ENABLED` / `REFTRIX_EMBEDDING_CACHE_ROOT` / `REFTRIX_EMBEDDING_CACHE_ROOT_ALLOW_FALLBACK`, package-level; ADR-0040) — branch `fix/embedding-cache-temp-leak`; 2026-06-08** / **Embedding キャッシュ env var (パッケージレベル; ADR-0040)**:
  - **EN — Added**: 3 additive env vars consumed by `src/services/layout-embedding.service.ts`: `EMBEDDING_CACHE_ENABLED` (default true; read in `initializeEmbeddingCache` — `process.env.EMBEDDING_CACHE_ENABLED === "false"` disables when `config.enabled` is unspecified), `REFTRIX_EMBEDDING_CACHE_ROOT` (default `/tmp/reftrix-embedding-cache`; `resolveEmbeddingCacheRoot()` rejects roots outside the `os.tmpdir()` allowlist by default + rejects null-byte/`..`), and `REFTRIX_EMBEDDING_CACHE_ROOT_ALLOW_FALLBACK` (`"true"` opts into degrade-to-default-root + `logger.warn`). All defaults unchanged → no breaking change. AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date 2026-06-08.
  - **JP — 追加**: `src/services/layout-embedding.service.ts` が消費する additive env var 3 つ: `EMBEDDING_CACHE_ENABLED` (default true; `initializeEmbeddingCache` で読み取り — `config.enabled` 未指定時に `process.env.EMBEDDING_CACHE_ENABLED === "false"` で無効化)、`REFTRIX_EMBEDDING_CACHE_ROOT` (default `/tmp/reftrix-embedding-cache`; `resolveEmbeddingCacheRoot()` が `os.tmpdir()` allowlist 外 root を default reject + null-byte/`..` reject)、`REFTRIX_EMBEDDING_CACHE_ROOT_ALLOW_FALLBACK` (`"true"` で default root への degrade + `logger.warn` を opt-in)。default 全不変 → breaking change なし。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日 2026-06-08。

- **backfill `queued`-stuck rescue — Section C worker-present recovery (package-level; ADR-0007 Amendment 2 `queued`-Stuck Rescue Lifecycle) — working-tree (branch `feature/backfill-worker-autostart`; IO Impl Decision V1 = APPROVE `019e7e6d-0da2`; 2026-05-31; T+1d 2026-06-01)** / **backfill `queued` 固着救済 — Section C worker 稼働時リカバリ (パッケージレベル; ADR-0007 Amendment 2 `queued` 固着 rescue ライフサイクル)**:
  - **EN — Added**: Added Section C (`reconcileQueuedRows`) to `src/services/backfill-reconciliation.service.ts` — a worker-present rescue scan for `embeddingBackfillStatus='queued'` rows whose BullMQ job was lost. P2 rescue re-arms (`queued`→`queued`, `startedAt=now()`, `retryCount`+1, re-enqueue all 7 categories) emitting `backfill_rescue_queued` (`actor=system:backfill-reconciliation-cron`, details `{retryCountAfter, stalenessMs, enqueuedCategories}`, `result:"failure"`); P3 give-up CAS `queued`→`failed_with_known_reason`+`supervisor_restart_orphan` at `retryCount>=SKIP_RECOVERY_RETRY_CAP=5`, emitting `backfill_rescue_queued_gave_up` (details `{failureReason, originStatus:"queued", retryCountAtCap}`). `src/services/worker-supervisor-lifecycle.service.ts` give-up (terminal) scan corrected to a from-status 2-branch (`queued`-origin → `failed_with_known_reason`+`supervisor_restart_orphan` recovery-IN; `in_progress`-origin → bare `failed`+`vision_unload_timeout` recovery-OUT). `src/audit/audit-actions.ts` adds the 2 SSOT action constants `AUDIT_ACTION_BACKFILL_RESCUE_QUEUED` + `AUDIT_ACTION_BACKFILL_RESCUE_QUEUED_GAVE_UP` (branch total `audit-actions.ts` delta vs `f92abf36` is +5, incl. 3 co-located deferred-spawn-closure actions; INV-AUDIT-EMIT-SSOT-IMPORT-001 production bare literal 0). No enum change (INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 unaffected). Standing regression: new `tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-backfill-queued-rescue-014.test.ts` (INV-PAGE-QUEUE-001, 10 contracts, pass^3) + `tests/regression/standing/worker-lifecycle/inv-backfill-secondary-spawn-timeout-terminal-013.test.ts` strengthened 4→5 tests (from-status 2-branch co-located OLD `queued`+OLD `in_progress`, M-1 closure). IO ground-truth full 4-domain standing: 113 files / 765 passed / 4 skipped / 0 failed; worker-lifecycle pass^3 3/3 (403 each). Both new `audit_logs` actions: 365-day retention, PII-free numeric/enum `details`, `truncateAuditTargetId` (CWE-209), GDPR Art.30.
  - **JP — 追加**: `src/services/backfill-reconciliation.service.ts` に Section C (`reconcileQueuedRows`) を追加 — BullMQ ジョブを失った `embeddingBackfillStatus='queued'` 行の worker 稼働時 rescue scan。P2 rescue は re-arm (`queued`→`queued`、`startedAt=now()`、`retryCount`+1、7 カテゴリ全 re-enqueue) し `backfill_rescue_queued` (`actor=system:backfill-reconciliation-cron`、details `{retryCountAfter, stalenessMs, enqueuedCategories}`、`result:"failure"`) を emit; P3 give-up は `retryCount>=SKIP_RECOVERY_RETRY_CAP=5` 時に CAS `queued`→`failed_with_known_reason`+`supervisor_restart_orphan` し `backfill_rescue_queued_gave_up` (details `{failureReason, originStatus:"queued", retryCountAtCap}`) を emit。`src/services/worker-supervisor-lifecycle.service.ts` の give-up (terminal) scan を from-status 2分岐に訂正 (`queued`-origin → `failed_with_known_reason`+`supervisor_restart_orphan` recovery-IN; `in_progress`-origin → bare `failed`+`vision_unload_timeout` recovery-OUT)。`src/audit/audit-actions.ts` に SSOT action 定数 2 個 `AUDIT_ACTION_BACKFILL_RESCUE_QUEUED` + `AUDIT_ACTION_BACKFILL_RESCUE_QUEUED_GAVE_UP` を追加 (branch の `audit-actions.ts` `f92abf36` 比 total delta は +5、co-located な deferred-spawn-closure 系 action 3 個を含む; INV-AUDIT-EMIT-SSOT-IMPORT-001 production bare literal 0)。enum 変更なし (INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 非影響)。standing regression: 新規 `tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-backfill-queued-rescue-014.test.ts` (INV-PAGE-QUEUE-001、10 契約、pass^3) + `tests/regression/standing/worker-lifecycle/inv-backfill-secondary-spawn-timeout-terminal-013.test.ts` を 4→5 tests に強化 (from-status 2分岐の co-located OLD `queued`+OLD `in_progress`、M-1 closure)。IO ground-truth full 4-domain standing: 113 files / 765 passed / 4 skipped / 0 failed; worker-lifecycle pass^3 3/3 (各 403)。両 `audit_logs` action とも 365 日保持、PII-free numeric/enum `details`、`truncateAuditTargetId` (CWE-209)、GDPR Art.30。

### Removed / 削除

- **PR-SS-B — Screenshot TTL structure removal + H-1 inline GDPR-deletion fallback + INV-SCREENSHOT-RETENTION-001 (package-level; ADR-0041; deletion-heavy ~60%) — worktree (branch `worktree-agent-aa7ff1f3794322c19`, base `feature/webui` `15c2dc7d`; IO Impl Decision = APPROVE anchor `019ebfbb`; commits `bc0c4a89` Red → `e5327503` Green → `973fcf48` Refactor; 2026-06-13; T+1d 2026-06-14)** / **PR-SS-B — Screenshot TTL 構造撤去 + H-1 inline GDPR 削除 fallback + INV-SCREENSHOT-RETENTION-001 (パッケージレベル; ADR-0041; 削除支配 ~60%)**:
  - **EN — Removed / Changed**: Structurally removed the Screenshot TTL cron from `src/services/screenshot-persistence.service.ts` (deleted the `src/cron/screenshot-cleanup-cron.ts` module, the `cleanupExpired` orchestrator + helpers, the `screenshot_ttl_cleanup` audit emit) and added an **H-1 inline GDPR-deletion fallback** to `src/services/gdpr-deletion.service.ts` (`buildScreenshotPrismaAdapter` parameter-bound raw-SQL adapter + `getScreenshotService` fallback). Screenshot retention is now **"until `data.delete`"** (ADR-0041 Decision 3/7). GDPR Art.17 deletion no longer depends on DI wiring (strengthened, not weakened). **No production runtime behavior change to the `data.delete` happy path** — H-1 only changes the factory-unwired path from graceful-skip to structural deletion.
  - **JP — 削除 / 変更**: `src/services/screenshot-persistence.service.ts` から Screenshot TTL cron を構造ごと撤去 (`src/cron/screenshot-cleanup-cron.ts` module 削除、`cleanupExpired` orchestrator + helpers、`screenshot_ttl_cleanup` audit emit を撤去) し、`src/services/gdpr-deletion.service.ts` に **H-1 inline GDPR 削除 fallback** を追加 (`buildScreenshotPrismaAdapter` パラメータバインド付き raw-SQL アダプタ + `getScreenshotService` fallback)。Screenshot 保持は **「`data.delete` まで」** (ADR-0041 Decision 3/7)。GDPR Art.17 削除は DI 配線非依存に (強化、弱体化なし)。**`data.delete` happy path の production runtime 挙動変更なし** — H-1 は factory 未配線経路を graceful-skip から構造的削除へ変えるのみ。

### Changed / 変更

- **Search embedding-failure response contract — fail-loud `success:false` leaf + per-service `degradedServices` aggregator (package-level; ADR-0043; PR-2a + PR-2a-2 + PR-2b; breaking response contract; no env var) — working-tree (branch `feature/webui`, HEAD `ad8e1d51`; IO Impl Decision PR-2a `019ec70d` / PR-2a-2 `019ec764` / PR-2b `019ec84f`; 2026-06-15; T+1d 2026-06-16)** / **検索 embedding 障害応答契約 — fail-loud `success:false` leaf + service 単位 `degradedServices` aggregator (パッケージレベル; ADR-0043; PR-2a + PR-2a-2 + PR-2b; breaking 応答契約; env var なし)**:
  - **EN — Changed (breaking response contract)**: The `@reftrixmcp/mcp-server` package implements the ADR-0043 leaf-aggregator fail-loud contract, replacing the silent `success:true total:0` masking of an embedding-infrastructure outage. **PR-2a (motion + part leaf)**: motion is the 5th fail-loud embedding-required leaf (inline swallow removed → `success:false`); `part.search` becomes a 4-state consumer with the new public `searchPartsFulltext(query, options)` for `search_mode=text` full-text fallback (`success:true`) vs visual/hybrid `success:false`. **PR-2a-2 (layout + background + responsive leaf)**: the 3 remaining leaves unify to `success:false` + `degradedReason` on embedding failure, closing the transient mixed-state. **PR-2b (search.unified aggregator)**: the aggregator surfaces an additive `data.degradedServices: [{service, reason}]` instead of silently dropping a failed leaf to `[]`, returning `success:false` only on all-embedding-required-fail. **F-2B-L-08 (degraded-path logSearch)**: PR-2b's degraded path newly calls `logSearch` (`src/tools/search-unified.tool.ts`), but the logged `query` is the **same existing 200-char `search_logs` truncate** — NOT a new PII surface (CWE-209 / GDPR Art.5(1)(c) clean; degradedReason markers are enum-only). Two-layer SSOT: `src/services/_shared/resolve-query-embedding.ts` (service) + `src/tools/_shared/embedding-failure-response.ts` (tool). Covered by 3 large-page standing INVs (`INV-MOTION-EMBEDDING-FAILLOUD-SINGLE-ATTEMPT-001` 7/7 / `INV-LEAF-EMBEDDING-FAILLOUT-001` 15/15 / `INV-AGGREGATOR-DEGRADED-001` 11/11). **No env var / DB migration / MCP tool name / audit action / enum change.** The published `@reftrixmcp/mcp-server` runtime dependency tree / SBOM is unchanged. AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-15.
  - **JP — 変更 (breaking 応答契約)**: `@reftrixmcp/mcp-server` パッケージが ADR-0043 の leaf-aggregator fail-loud 契約を実装し、embedding インフラ障害の silent な `success:true total:0` 偽装を撤去する。**PR-2a (motion + part leaf)**: motion を 5 番目の fail-loud embedding 必須 leaf 化 (inline swallow 撤去 → `success:false`)、`part.search` を 4-state consumer 化し新 public `searchPartsFulltext(query, options)` で `search_mode=text` の fulltext fallback (`success:true`) と visual/hybrid `success:false` を区別。**PR-2a-2 (layout + background + responsive leaf)**: 残り 3 leaf を embedding 障害時 `success:false` + `degradedReason` に統一し transient mixed-state を解消。**PR-2b (search.unified aggregator)**: aggregator が失敗 leaf を `[]` に silent drop せず additive な `data.degradedServices: [{service, reason}]` を surface、embedding 必須全滅時のみ `success:false`。**F-2B-L-08 (degraded-path logSearch)**: PR-2b の degraded path は `logSearch` を新規 call する (`src/tools/search-unified.tool.ts`) が、記録 `query` は `search_logs` の**既存 200 字 truncate と同一** — 新 PII surface ではない (CWE-209 / GDPR Art.5(1)(c) clean; degradedReason marker は enum のみ)。2 層 SSOT: `src/services/_shared/resolve-query-embedding.ts` (service) + `src/tools/_shared/embedding-failure-response.ts` (tool)。3 件の large-page standing INV (`INV-MOTION-EMBEDDING-FAILLOUD-SINGLE-ATTEMPT-001` 7/7 / `INV-LEAF-EMBEDDING-FAILLOUT-001` 15/15 / `INV-AGGREGATOR-DEGRADED-001` 11/11) でカバー。**env var / DB migration / MCP tool 名 / audit action / enum 変更なし。** 公開 `@reftrixmcp/mcp-server` runtime 依存ツリー / SBOM は不変。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日: 2026-06-15。

### Fixed / 修正

- **EN — Fixed**: The production fix (the worker-thread device gate) lives in the `@reftrixmcp/ml` package — see the root + internal CHANGELOGs for the full entry. For `@reftrixmcp/mcp-server`, the relevant per-file delta is **NEW** `tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-embedding-worker-cuda-gate-001.test.ts` (`INV-EMBEDDING-WORKER-CUDA-GATE-001`, large-page domain, 8 cases): Layer (i) helper unit ×5 (`resolveWorkerEffectiveDevice` honors the resolved provider, never falls back to `config.device`; `canSwitchToCuda` ANDs `verifyCudaAvailability` + `isLdLibraryPathSetAtOsLevel`), a CWE-209 `.so`-non-disclosure assert ×1, and Layer (iii) AST source-pin ×2 against `packages/ml/src/embeddings/worker-thread.ts` (the `resolveWorkerEffectiveDevice` call present + the old `config.device` ternary absent). The standing test imports the production helper module, so a gate regression fails it RED in CI; IO verified it 8/8 GREEN and non-vacuous (re-injecting the `config.device` ternary REDs the AST-pin). The incident was that MCP search returned 0 results for every type on a CUDA-EP-`.so`-absent host whose `ONNX_EXECUTION_PROVIDER=cuda` env let `worker-thread.ts` pass an unbacked `device:"cuda"` to transformers (native `dlopen()` raw-throw). **No `@reftrixmcp/mcp-server` source/dist code was changed** (the production fix is in `@reftrixmcp/ml`); no new env var, no DB migration, no audit action, no enum change (schema-enum-sync unaffected), no MCP tool change; `vitest` / `@vitest/coverage-v8` and the published runtime dependency tree / SBOM are unaffected. AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date: 2026-06-14.
