- **page.analyze same-URL near-concurrent resubmit race — URL-stable UUIDv5 jobId dedup (PR-SAMEURL-DEDUP, package-level) — working-tree (branch `fix/page-analyze-same-url-dedup`, base main `25c1480a`; IO Impl Decision V1 = APPROVE `019e7967-963f-75ef-af6e-0916bf7342dc`; 2026-06-02; T+1d 2026-06-03)** / **page.analyze 同一 URL 近接多重 submit race — URL-stable UUIDv5 jobId dedup (PR-SAMEURL-DEDUP、パッケージレベル)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Unified the page-analyze BullMQ jobId from the per-call `webPageId = uuidv7()` to a URL-derived stable identifier so near-concurrent same-URL submits collide on the collision-guard claim key and dedup at the enqueue layer, eliminating the `"Missing key for job"` clobber (ADR-0018 Amendment 11). New helper `buildUrlStableJobId(url) = uuidv5(normalizeUrlForValidation(url), UUID_NAMESPACE_PAGE_ANALYZE_URL)` (`queues/page-analyze-queue.ts:46/71`), where the namespace is the `uuid` package (v14.0.0) predefined `v5.URL` constant `6ba7b811-9dad-11d1-80b4-00c04fd430c8` (no fabricated magic UUID); `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` now sets `jobId: buildUrlStableJobId(data.url)` (`:879`). UUIDv5 is a valid RFC 4122 UUID, so the async / polling schema gates (`tools/page/output.schemas.ts:1498/1527/1725` `z.string().uuid()` + `get-job-status.tool.ts:76` MCP `format:"uuid"`) pass without relaxation. The async response now returns the URL-stable UUIDv5 `jobId` (polling preserved) plus an additive `webPageId` field. Per-file production LoC delta: `queues/page-analyze-queue.ts` +57/-3 (helper + jobId swap + 2 comment corrections at L819/L870) / `tools/page/analyze.tool.ts` +12/-8 (response jobId UUIDv5 + additive webPageId) / `tools/page/output.schemas.ts` +14/-1 (additive webPageId field + L1498 comment correction). Test LoC delta: `tests/queues/page-analyze-queue.test.ts` +54/-2 (helper determinism / version=5 / uuid-validate unit tests) + `tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-page-queue-001-e-jobid-concurrent.test.ts` +35/-16 (C16 URL-distinctness pin). New standing regression `tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-page-sameurl-dedup-001.test.ts` (`INV-PAGE-SAMEURL-DEDUP-001`, large-page, 392 LoC, **10 tests**, 3-block: A round-trip / B ≤1 survivor / C Layer-2; real Redis + real Prisma). On Redis-unreachable the guard fails open (`enqueued_fail_open`, accepted-risk). No new production audit emit; `truncateId(8)` 32-bit UUIDv5 fragment in `enqueued_retry` details is non-identifying (CWE-209). AGPL §5(a)/(b) modification notice date 2026-06-02.
  - **JP — 修正**: page-analyze の BullMQ jobId を per-call `webPageId = uuidv7()` から URL 派生 stable 識別子に統一し、近接同時の同一 URL submit が collision-guard claim key で衝突して enqueue 層で dedup されるようにして `"Missing key for job"` clobber を排除 (ADR-0018 Amendment 11)。新 helper `buildUrlStableJobId(url) = uuidv5(normalizeUrlForValidation(url), UUID_NAMESPACE_PAGE_ANALYZE_URL)` (`queues/page-analyze-queue.ts:46/71`)、namespace は `uuid` package (v14.0.0) の predefined `v5.URL` 定数 `6ba7b811-9dad-11d1-80b4-00c04fd430c8` (magic UUID 捏造なし); `addPageAnalyzeJobWithGuard` は `jobId: buildUrlStableJobId(data.url)` を設定する (`:879`)。UUIDv5 は valid RFC 4122 UUID のため async / polling schema gate (`tools/page/output.schemas.ts:1498/1527/1725` `z.string().uuid()` + `get-job-status.tool.ts:76` MCP `format:"uuid"`) を relax 不要で通過。async response は URL-stable UUIDv5 `jobId` を返す (polling 無傷) + additive `webPageId` field 新設。per-file production LoC delta: `queues/page-analyze-queue.ts` +57/-3 (helper + jobId 差替 + L819/L870 comment 2 箇所訂正) / `tools/page/analyze.tool.ts` +12/-8 (response jobId UUIDv5 + additive webPageId) / `tools/page/output.schemas.ts` +14/-1 (additive webPageId field + L1498 comment 訂正)。test LoC delta: `tests/queues/page-analyze-queue.test.ts` +54/-2 (helper 決定性 / version=5 / uuid-validate unit test) + `tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-page-queue-001-e-jobid-concurrent.test.ts` +35/-16 (C16 URL-distinctness pin)。新 standing regression `tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-page-sameurl-dedup-001.test.ts` (`INV-PAGE-SAMEURL-DEDUP-001`、large-page、392 LoC、**10 tests**、3-block: A round-trip / B ≤1 survivor / C Layer-2; real Redis + real Prisma)。Redis 到達不能時は guard が fail-open (`enqueued_fail_open`、accepted-risk)。新 production audit emit なし; `enqueued_retry` details の `truncateId(8)` 32-bit UUIDv5 fragment は non-identifying (CWE-209)。AGPL §5(a)/(b) 改変通知日 2026-06-02。

- **section_visual blank / no-position backfill terminal-skip — large-page overflow sections reach `completed` (3→5 skip reasons, package-level) — working-tree (branch `feature/backfill-worker-autostart`; IO Impl Decision V1 = APPROVE `019e7f6c-9ff2`; 2026-06-01; T+1d 2026-06-02)** / **section_visual blank / no-position backfill terminal-skip — large-page の overflow section が `completed` に到達 (skip reason 3→5、パッケージレベル)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Closed a structural stall where backfill-path (`fallbackEnabled === false`) section-visual embeddings that are rendered-but-empty (`isBlank === true`) or lack crop geometry (no `sectionPositionMap` position, or `height < 10`) stayed perpetually pending so the page never reached `completed`. Per-file LoC delta (vs base, total +143/-8): `phases/types.ts` +90 (T1 SSOT `EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` 23→25, derived `EMBEDDING_SECTION_VISUAL_SKIP_REASONS` 3→5, `SECTION_VISUAL_TERMINAL_REASON_CANDIDATES` adds the 2 new literals; "23-value" count strings dropped from comments), `phases/phase-5-embedding.ts` +59 (2 new terminal exits at `processSingleSectionVisualEmbedding` lines ~2202 `section_visual_no_position` / ~2274 `section_visual_blank`; `writeSectionVisionSkipReason` JSDoc 2→4 exits), `page-analyze-worker.ts` +20 (backfill-path marker wiring). New enum values (2): `section_visual_blank`, `section_visual_no_position` — both map to backfill status `not_required` via `skipReasonToBackfillStatus()`, NOT added to `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_FAILURE_REASONS`. New migration: `packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260531090000_add_section_visual_blank_no_position_skip_reasons/migration.sql` (CHECK 5-value via DROP/ADD CONSTRAINT, partial index preserved, non-breaking, DB-applied). New standing regression `inv-section-visual-blank-terminal-015.test.ts` (`INV-SECTION-VISUAL-BLANK-TERMINAL-015`, large-page, +323 LoC) + `INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` section-visual 4-site lockstep updated to 5-value. Both new values are non-PII degraded-coverage technical terminals (GDPR Art.4(1) non-applicable), distinct from `section_visual_pii_excluded`.
  - **JP — 修正**: backfill path (`fallbackEnabled === false`) で、描画済だが空 (`isBlank === true`) または crop geometry 欠落 (`sectionPositionMap` に position 無し、または `height < 10`) の section-visual embedding が永久 pending に滞留し、ページが `completed` に到達しない構造的 stall を closure。per-file LoC delta (base 比、total +143/-8): `phases/types.ts` +90 (T1 SSOT `EMBEDDING_SKIP_REASONS` 23→25、derived `EMBEDDING_SECTION_VISUAL_SKIP_REASONS` 3→5、`SECTION_VISUAL_TERMINAL_REASON_CANDIDATES` に新 2 literal 追加; comment の "23-value" 件数表記を除去)、`phases/phase-5-embedding.ts` +59 (`processSingleSectionVisualEmbedding` line ~2202 `section_visual_no_position` / ~2274 `section_visual_blank` の 2 新 terminal exit; `writeSectionVisionSkipReason` JSDoc 2→4 exits)、`page-analyze-worker.ts` +20 (backfill-path marker 配線)。新 enum 値 (2): `section_visual_blank`、`section_visual_no_position` — いずれも `skipReasonToBackfillStatus()` で backfill status `not_required` にマップ、`EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_FAILURE_REASONS` には追加しない。新 migration: `packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260531090000_add_section_visual_blank_no_position_skip_reasons/migration.sql` (DROP/ADD CONSTRAINT で CHECK 5値、partial index 維持、non-breaking、DB 適用済)。新 standing regression `inv-section-visual-blank-terminal-015.test.ts` (`INV-SECTION-VISUAL-BLANK-TERMINAL-015`、large-page、+323 LoC) + `INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004` section-visual 4-site lockstep を 5値に更新。新 2 値はいずれも非 PII の degraded-coverage technical terminal (GDPR Art.4(1) 非該当) であり、`section_visual_pii_excluded` とは意味が異なる。

- **backfill worker auto-start — deferred-spawn retry + scan-based terminal closure (package-level; ADR-0011 Amendment 7) — working-tree (branch `feature/backfill-worker-autostart`; IO Impl Decision V1 = APPROVE `019e7967-963f-75ef-af6e-0916bf7342dc`; 2026-05-31; T+1d 2026-06-01)** / **backfill worker 自動起動 — deferred-spawn リトライ + scan-based terminal closure (パッケージレベル; ADR-0011 Amendment 7)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Closed the structural stall (MEMORY.md #162) where large-page overflow embeddings delegated to the secondary `embedding-backfill` worker never reach a terminal state when the worker fails to spawn. `worker-supervisor-lifecycle.service.ts` (+336 LoC) adds a bounded deferred-spawn retry timer (`SECONDARY_SPAWN_RETRY_CADENCE_MS=30000`, `SECONDARY_SPAWN_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS=600000`, `SECONDARY_SPAWN_RETRY_MAX_ATTEMPTS=20=ceil(600000/30000)`, `SECONDARY_SPAWN_RETRY_PROBE_FAILED_STRIKE_LIMIT=3`, all derived from existing SSOT constants), a 3-strike `vision_probe_unavailable` block, and a fallback-on-absence scan-based terminal `updateMany` (`{in:['queued','in_progress']} AND startedAt<now-10min → 'failed'/'vision_unload_timeout'`). `audit-actions.ts` (+100 LoC) adds the SSOT action constants `AUDIT_ACTION_VISION_PROBE_UNAVAILABLE`, `AUDIT_ACTION_BACKFILL_SECONDARY_SPAWN_TIMEOUT`, and co-migrated `AUDIT_ACTION_WORKER_LOCK_TTL_FALLBACK` + `AUDIT_ACTOR_WORKER_SUPERVISOR`; `worker-supervisor-helpers.ts` (+5/-? LoC) imports the SSOT actor constant (INV-AUDIT-EMIT-SSOT-IMPORT-001, zero production bare literals). New standing regression: `inv-backfill-secondary-spawn-retry-011.test.ts` (+394 LoC, 10 tests) + `inv-backfill-secondary-spawn-timeout-terminal-013.test.ts` (+348 LoC, 4 tests). Full standing regression (4 domains): 112 files / 754 passed / 0 failed (IO ground-truth). New `audit_logs` actions: `vision_probe_unavailable` (targetType `worker`, details `{attemptCount, probeFailedStreak}`), `backfill_secondary_spawn_timeout` (targetType `web_page`, details `{attemptCount, elapsedMs, finalProbeStatus, terminalizedCount}`), both `actor=system:worker-supervisor`, 365-day retention, PII-free.
  - **JP — 修正**: secondary `embedding-backfill` worker に委譲された large-page overflow embeddings が worker spawn 失敗時に terminal 状態へ到達しない構造的 stall (MEMORY.md #162) を closure。`worker-supervisor-lifecycle.service.ts` (+336 LoC) に bounded deferred-spawn retry timer (`SECONDARY_SPAWN_RETRY_CADENCE_MS=30000`、`SECONDARY_SPAWN_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS=600000`、`SECONDARY_SPAWN_RETRY_MAX_ATTEMPTS=20=ceil(600000/30000)`、`SECONDARY_SPAWN_RETRY_PROBE_FAILED_STRIKE_LIMIT=3`、いずれも既存 SSOT 定数から derive)、3-strike `vision_probe_unavailable` block、fallback-on-absence scan-based terminal `updateMany` (`{in:['queued','in_progress']} AND startedAt<now-10min → 'failed'/'vision_unload_timeout'`) を追加。`audit-actions.ts` (+100 LoC) に SSOT action 定数 `AUDIT_ACTION_VISION_PROBE_UNAVAILABLE`、`AUDIT_ACTION_BACKFILL_SECONDARY_SPAWN_TIMEOUT`、co-migration `AUDIT_ACTION_WORKER_LOCK_TTL_FALLBACK` + `AUDIT_ACTOR_WORKER_SUPERVISOR` を追加; `worker-supervisor-helpers.ts` (+5 LoC) は SSOT actor 定数を import (INV-AUDIT-EMIT-SSOT-IMPORT-001、production bare literal 0)。新 standing regression: `inv-backfill-secondary-spawn-retry-011.test.ts` (+394 LoC、10 tests) + `inv-backfill-secondary-spawn-timeout-terminal-013.test.ts` (+348 LoC、4 tests)。full standing regression (4 domain): 112 files / 754 passed / 0 failed (IO ground-truth)。新 `audit_logs` action: `vision_probe_unavailable` (targetType `worker`、details `{attemptCount, probeFailedStreak}`)、`backfill_secondary_spawn_timeout` (targetType `web_page`、details `{attemptCount, elapsedMs, finalProbeStatus, terminalizedCount}`)、いずれも `actor=system:worker-supervisor`、365日保持、PII-free。

- **main CI-green hotfix — standing test CI-only flake determinised (package-level; INV-PHASE5-COLDLOAD-DELTA-EXCLUSION-001 branch 4, test-only) — working-tree (branch `fix/main-ci-standing-coldload-and-tmp-audit`; IO Impl Decision V0 = APPROVE; 2026-05-30; T+1d 2026-05-31)** / **main CI-green hotfix — standing test の CI-only flake を決定論化 (パッケージレベル; INV-PHASE5-COLDLOAD-DELTA-EXCLUSION-001 branch 4、テストのみ)**:
  - **EN — Fixed (test-only)**: Determinised the `INV-PHASE5-COLDLOAD-DELTA-EXCLUSION-001` standing test (large-page domain) CI-only flake by `vi.spyOn`-stubbing `checkMemoryPressure` to evaluate the mocked rss against fixed dev-baseline thresholds (12288/14336MB) — host-RAM-independent. Root cause: a host-RAM-dependent memory-pressure threshold (`totalMb*0.7`) evaluated against a fixed mocked absolute rss bifurcated branch 4 (loop-head 3× budget anomalous arena) between dev box (`[0,1,2]` PASS) and small CI runners (`[0]` fail). Production code unchanged; expected `[0,1,2]` unchanged. IO direct standing pass 6/6.

- **PR-PART30CAP — Phase 5 part-embedding partial-completion residual enqueue gap fix (package-level details, BUG-1, dual-trigger) — commit `d7536724` (baseline `fafe1677`; IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e7225-812e` APPROVE; ADR-0007 Amendment 2 Accepted; 2026-05-30; T+1d 2026-05-31)** / **PR-PART30CAP — Phase 5 part-embedding partial-completion residual enqueue gap 修正 (パッケージレベル詳細、BUG-1、dual-trigger)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Extended the part_text / part_visual backfill enqueue trigger to `(partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD) OR (inline partial-completion residual pending > 0)`, closing the parts≤100 enqueue gap that left heavy CPU pages stuck perpetually `in_progress`. Pending determined via a single `collectCategoryPendingSnapshot` call (no N+1) at `dispatchBackfillJobsForPage`; `resolveBackfillDispatchCategories` receives a bool `hasPendingParts` flag (no DB argument; INV-007 Block A pure-function unit-pin preserved). `PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD = 100` decoupled from the enqueue trigger but retained as an inline-cap semantic.
  - **JP — 修正**: part_text / part_visual backfill enqueue trigger を `(partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD) OR (inline partial-completion residual pending > 0)` に拡張し、parts≤100 page を永続 `in_progress` で stuck させていた enqueue gap を closure。pending は `dispatchBackfillJobsForPage` で `collectCategoryPendingSnapshot` 1-call (N+1 なし) から判定; `resolveBackfillDispatchCategories` は bool `hasPendingParts` flag を受け取る (DB 引数なし; INV-007 Block A pure-function unit-pin 維持)。`PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD = 100` は enqueue trigger から直交化するが inline-cap semantic として残存。

- **PR-BACKFILL-PAUSE-RACE — Stage 2 `worker.pause(true)` formal removal (package-level details, BullMQ Lua race H2 + event-loop pause race H3 structural elimination) — commit `49013161` (baseline `b2f6f8b8`; IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e6f95-0428` APPROVE; ADR-0034 Amendment 5 + ADR-0009 §Status further retract; 2026-05-28; T+1d 2026-05-29)** / **PR-BACKFILL-PAUSE-RACE — Stage 2 `worker.pause(true)` 正式 removal (パッケージレベル詳細、BullMQ Lua race H2 + event-loop pause race H3 構造的消滅)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Removed `await worker.pause(true)` from both worker success paths (ADR-0034 §Decision 1 Stage 2 formal retirement), structurally eliminating the BullMQ `moveToCompleted` Lua `paused`-flag race (H2) and the event-loop starvation pause-related machinery race (H3). `applyPostJobLifecycleGate` retired to a `Promise<void>` no-op stub for legacy test caller backward compat (IO Plan Decision V1 §Conflict 2 ruling (a)); `applyPostJobMemoryGate` becomes the sole post-job gate with explicit `WORKER_MAX_JOBS_BEFORE_RESTART=0` fail-closed contract (SEC-PLAN-V0-M-01 closure). New AST gate `verify-no-worker-pause.mjs` enforces zero `worker.pause(` callsites in production with 3-bypass-pattern reject (SEC-PLAN-V0-M-02 closure). Orthogonal to ADR-0035 §Decision 1 (H1, active maintained).

- **Embedding backfill — terminal failure status `failed_with_known_reason` unification + motion backfill DI fix (package-level details) — working-tree (branch `fix/backfill-terminal-section-visual`, baseline `ffbfd725`; IO Impl Decision anchor `019e6a93-bcb0` CONDITIONAL; 2026-05-28)** / **Embedding backfill — terminal 失敗 status `failed_with_known_reason` 統一 + motion backfill DI 修正 (パッケージレベル詳細)**:
  - **EN — Fixed (defect B)**: Terminal backfill failure now writes `failed_with_known_reason` + `failure_reason` + `failed_at` (reconciliation → `supervisor_restart_orphan`, worker job-exception → `stall_timeout`), both `auto_recoverable`, instead of metadata-less plain `failed`. Closes the `failure_reason`/`failed_at` NULL observability gap and routes rows into the recovery scan window.
  - **EN — Fixed (defect A)**: Registered `setFramePrismaClientFactory` in the fork-child DI setup so motion backfill `saveMotionEmbedding` resolves its module-scoped Prisma client factory.
  - **JP — 修正 (defect B)**: terminal backfill 失敗が、metadata 無しの plain `failed` の代わりに `failed_with_known_reason` + `failure_reason` + `failed_at` (reconciliation → `supervisor_restart_orphan`、worker job 例外 → `stall_timeout`、いずれも `auto_recoverable`) を書き込むように修正。`failure_reason`/`failed_at` NULL の observability 欠落を closure し、recovery scan window に乗せる。
  - **JP — 修正 (defect A)**: fork-child DI setup に `setFramePrismaClientFactory` を登録し、motion backfill `saveMotionEmbedding` が module-scoped Prisma client factory を解決できるように修正。
  - **Per-file production deltas (`git diff --numstat ffbfd725`, added/removed) / per-file production 差分**:

### Changed / 変更

- **PR-BT-5 — Phase 5 per-sub-phase fork-only model + recycle-disable fix (M-1-RSS structural fix, package-level details) — working-tree (baseline `05f91d18`; IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e65a3-aaf7` CONDITIONAL / documentation-only check; ADR-0039 Accepted; 2026-05-27; T+1d 2026-05-28)** / **PR-BT-5 — Phase 5 per-sub-phase fork-only model + recycle-disable 修正 (M-1-RSS 構造修正, パッケージレベル詳細)**:
  - **EN — Changed**: Phase 5 embedding migrated from the 2-child (text/visual) fork model to a **per-sub-phase fork-only model** (ADR-0039 Decision 1) — ≤9 fully-separate `child_process.fork()` forks, each processing one sub-phase then `exit(0)` so the OS reclaims the whole arena. Roots out the inter-sub-phase e5-base reload arena accumulation that SIGKILL'd heavy CPU sites. Plus the recycle-disable fix (`PIPELINE_RECYCLE_THRESHOLD="0"` no-op inside the one-shot fork; decisive harm = the real-machine-measured C1-mask). Per-file production deltas below.
  - **JP — 変更**: Phase 5 embedding を 2-child (text/visual) fork model から **per-sub-phase fork-only model** に移行 (ADR-0039 Decision 1) — ≤9 完全分離 `child_process.fork()`、各々 1 sub-phase 処理 → `exit(0)` で OS が arena 全回収。heavy CPU site を SIGKILL させていた inter-sub-phase e5-base reload arena 累積を根絶。加えて recycle-disable 修正 (one-shot fork 内 `PIPELINE_RECYCLE_THRESHOLD="0"` no-op; decisive harm = 実機 measured な C1-mask)。per-file production delta は下記。

- **PR-BT-3 — `section_visual` fork-child throw-fallback reachability fix (makes the PR-BT-2 terminal-skip markers fire in production fork-only mode, package-level details) — `fix/backfill-terminal-section-visual` (HEAD `05f91d18` = PR-BT-2; PR-BT-3 working-tree; IO Impl Decision anchor `019e5b01` CONDITIONAL APPROVE; 2026-05-25; T+1d 2026-05-26)** / **PR-BT-3 — `section_visual` fork-child throw-fallback 到達性修正 (PR-BT-2 の terminal-skip marker を production fork-only mode で発火させる, パッケージレベル詳細)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Reachability fix — in production fork-only mode the `section_visual` fork-child called the text-only `backfillSectionVisualsForPage` wrapper (no DINOv2, no marker) and returned success, so `runForkOrFallback` returned the fork result and `SectionVisualProcessor.processInProcess` (DINOv2 + `writeSectionVisionSkipReason` marker path) was never reached → the PR-BT-2 markers never fired → uncroppable / dedup-skip pages stayed mis-pinned. The `section_visual` fork-child now `throw`s (mirroring `part_visual` per ADR-0015 §Decision 追補 1) so the catch-fallback routes to `processInProcess` → `runVisualEmbeddingSubPhases({ fallbackEnabled: false })` (persisted screenshot only, no per-section Playwright). Reuses the PR-BT-2 `section_embeddings.vision_skip_reason` column — no new migration. Per-file production deltas below.
  - **JP — 修正**: 到達性修正 — production fork-only mode で `section_visual` fork-child が text-only `backfillSectionVisualsForPage` wrapper (DINOv2 なし・marker なし) を呼び success を返したため、`runForkOrFallback` が fork 結果を返し `SectionVisualProcessor.processInProcess` (DINOv2 + `writeSectionVisionSkipReason` marker path) に到達せず → PR-BT-2 marker 未発火 → uncroppable / dedup-skip page が誤 pin のまま残った。`section_visual` fork-child は `throw` するようになり (ADR-0015 §Decision 追補 1 に従い `part_visual` を mirror)、catch-fallback が `processInProcess` → `runVisualEmbeddingSubPhases({ fallbackEnabled: false })` (永続 screenshot のみ、per-section Playwright なし) に routing する。PR-BT-2 の `section_embeddings.vision_skip_reason` 列を再利用 — 新 migration なし。per-file production delta は下記。
  - **Per-file production deltas / per-file production 差分**:
    - `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/phases/embedding-backfill-child.ts` — the `section_visual` fork-child case changed from calling the text-only service wrapper (returning success) to `throw` (mirroring the existing `part_visual` case at L322-342), with an explanatory comment citing ADR-0015 §Decision 追補 1 (the LCC-known-gap throw→in-process-fallback pattern); the orchestrator catch-fallback then routes to the in-process DINOv2 + terminal-skip-marker path. ~10 net new logic LoC (incl. comment). / `section_visual` fork-child case を text-only service wrapper 呼び出し (success を返す) から `throw` (既存 `part_visual` case L322-342 を mirror) に変更、ADR-0015 §Decision 追補 1 (LCC known-gap の throw→in-process-fallback パターン) を citation する説明 comment 付き; orchestrator catch-fallback が in-process DINOv2 + terminal-skip-marker path に routing する。~10 net new logic LoC (comment 含む)。

- **PR-BT-1 — Backfill dispatch completeness for 4 gate-less embedding categories (System-A correctness fix, package-level details) — `feature/pr-bt-1-backfill-dispatch-completeness` (commits 4ab2f257 + 73bdb8b9; IO Impl Decision anchor `019e5872` APPROVE; 2026-05-24; T+1d 2026-05-25)** / **PR-BT-1 — 4 gate-less embedding category の backfill dispatch completeness (系統A correctness 修正, パッケージレベル詳細)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: System-A correctness fix — `motion`/`background`/`js_animation`/`responsive` were never enqueued on the happy path (parity `pending > 0` → reconciliation cron mis-pinned the page to `failed`, i.e. "queue completed but DB=failed"). `dispatchBackfillJobsForPage` becomes a hybrid delegate; the `part_text`/`part_visual` `PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD` gate and the `section_visual` condition are PRESERVED; the 4 gate-less categories are unconditionally enqueued. **System-A only**; the System-B `section_visual` terminal-skip asymmetry is a separate follow-up (PR-BT-2), NOT included here. Per-file production deltas below.
  - **JP — 修正**: 系統A correctness 修正 — `motion`/`background`/`js_animation`/`responsive` が happy path で一度も enqueue されない (parity `pending > 0` → reconciliation cron がページを `failed` に誤 pin、= 「queue completed なのに DB=failed」)。`dispatchBackfillJobsForPage` を hybrid delegate 化、`part_text`/`part_visual` の `PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD` gate と `section_visual` 条件は PRESERVE、4 gate-less category を無条件 enqueue。**系統A のみ**。系統B の `section_visual` terminal-skip 非対称は別 follow-up (PR-BT-2)、本 PR には含まれない。per-file production delta は下記。
  - **Per-file production LoC deltas / per-file production LoC delta**:
    - `src/workers/page-analyze-worker.ts` (+185 / −28): adds the exported pure `resolveBackfillDispatchCategories(input)` which derives the gate-less category set as `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_CATEGORIES.filter((c) => !BACKFILL_DISPATCH_GATED_CATEGORIES.has(c))` (drift-proof SSOT), gates `part_text`/`part_visual` behind `Number.isFinite(partsSavedCount) && partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD` (gate PRESERVED), keeps `section_visual` under its `sections>0 && screenshot` condition, and unconditionally returns the 4 gate-less categories. The `dispatchBackfillJobsForPage` call-site gate is moved from `partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD` to `actualWebPageId` existence (so every completed page dispatches the gate-less set); the existing `addEmbeddingBackfillJobWithGuard` jobId-collision guard is reused. / exported pure `resolveBackfillDispatchCategories(input)` を追加し gate-less 集合を `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_CATEGORIES.filter((c) => !BACKFILL_DISPATCH_GATED_CATEGORIES.has(c))` で導出 (drift-proof SSOT)、`part_text`/`part_visual` を `Number.isFinite(partsSavedCount) && partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD` で gate (gate PRESERVE)、`section_visual` は `sections>0 && screenshot` 条件のまま、4 gate-less category を無条件 return。`dispatchBackfillJobsForPage` の call-site gate を `partsSavedCount > PART_SYNC_THRESHOLD` から `actualWebPageId` 存在に変更、既存 `addEmbeddingBackfillJobWithGuard` jobId-collision guard を再利用。

- **PR-1 GPU-COORD — Phase 5 CUDA selection gated on CUDA-EP availability (H regression fix, package-level details) — `feature/pr-1-gpu-coord-fork-child-vram-probe` (commit 85e8fb3b; IO Impl Decision V1 anchor `019e573c`; ADR-0038 HELD DRAFT; T+1d 2026-05-25)** / **PR-1 GPU-COORD — Phase 5 の CUDA 選択を CUDA-EP 可用性で gate (H regression 修正, パッケージレベル詳細)**:
  - **EN — Fixed**: Probe-side CUDA gate (`verifyCudaAvailability()`) + e5/text in-process device unification (`resolveInProcessDevice()`) so a CUDA-EP-absent host completes embedding on CPU instead of raw-throwing (164-failure H regression closure, FIND-IMPL-PR1-H-NEW-01).   - **JP — 修正**: probe 側 CUDA gate (`verifyCudaAvailability()`) + e5/text in-process device 統一 (`resolveInProcessDevice()`) により、CUDA-EP-absent host が raw throw せず embedding を CPU 完走 (164失敗 H regression closure、FIND-IMPL-PR1-H-NEW-01)。per-file production delta は下記。

    - **NEW env vars (Worker stderr management)**:
      - `REFTRIX_WORKER_STDERR_REDIRECT_ENABLED` (default `true`, P0.5 master opt-out).
      - `REFTRIX_WORKER_STDERR_CRON_INTERVAL_MS` (default `21600000` ms = 6h, range `3600000` (1h) .. `86400000` (24h hard upper bound via Zod refinement, NEW-U-11 L1).
      - `REFTRIX_WORKER_STDERR_SIZE_CAP_MB` (default `50`, Zod startup reject on overflow, NEW-U-11 L4).
      - `REFTRIX_WORKER_STDERR_DISK_PRESSURE_DEFENSE_ENABLED` (default `true`, emergency rollback toggle for L1-L4 only — Option (b) itself rolls back via `REFTRIX_WORKER_STDERR_REDIRECT_ENABLED=false`).
      - `REFTRIX_WORKER_STDERR_SANITIZE_STRICT_MODE` (default `false`, when `true` redacts all stderr content in no-content mode for paranoid PII protection per V1 §Risks "stderr file PII leakage" mitigation).

      - `EMBEDDING_DISPOSE_CEILING_MS` (default **5000**, range 100..60000ms) — `Promise.race` fail-open ceiling for `disposeEmbeddingPipeline` (ADR-0035 §Decision 1). `0` is debug-only legacy fallback (NOT recommended for production).

- **Wave 4 V4 Carryover Closure — package-level details (`feat/wave-4-v4-carryover-2026-05-13`; integration-owner Phase 3 docs-sync per IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e1e64-184c-7421-8c09-a82854086b10`; T+1d 2026-05-13)** [Wave-4-V4-2026-05-13] / **Wave 4 V4 carryover closure — パッケージレベル詳細 (`feat/wave-4-v4-carryover-2026-05-13`; integration-owner Phase 3 docs-sync per IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e1e64-184c-7421-8c09-a82854086b10`; T+1d 2026-05-13)** [Wave-4-V4-2026-05-13]:

  Package-level per-file LoC deltas + test counts + env var attestation per IO Impl Decision V0 anchor `019e1e64-184c-7421-8c09-a82854086b10` (CONDITIONAL APPROVE / documentation-only check). T+1d deadline 2026-05-13 today. The `apps/mcp-server/` scope changes consist of one source-file SSOT-derive migration (CO-21 closure) and two test-file Wave 5 partial `vi.mock` migrations.

  **CO-21 closure per-file delta**: `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/embedding-backfill-worker.ts` **+~5 LoC net** (line 833 `webPageId.slice(0, 8)` literal in the `job.log()` start interpolation migrated to `webPageId.slice(0, AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS.TARGET_ID_TRUNCATE_LENGTH)`; 4-line block comment added documenting Wave 5 LCC canonical CWE-209 PII Protection Pattern alignment per FIND-IMPL-LCC-PATCH-W5-02 anchor `019df7ab-2f5a`; remaining `slice(0, 8)` text only appears inside the explanatory comment, not in executable code). All `webPageId` truncation sites in the worker now derive from the single `AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS.TARGET_ID_TRUNCATE_LENGTH` SSOT, satisfying `docs:verify` §16 PII Truncation Literal Regression Gate (ADR-0032).

  **Test files Wave 5 partial `vi.mock` migration per-file delta** (2 files):
  - `apps/mcp-server/tests/workers/phases/shared/bbox-resolution.helper.test.ts` **+~12 LoC net**: `vi.mock("../../../../src/services/audit-log.service")` factory rewritten from object-literal `() => ({ getAuditLogService: () => ({ log: mockAuditLog }) })` to `async (importOriginal) => { const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import("../../../../src/services/audit-log.service")>(); return { ...actual, getAuditLogService: () => ({ log: mockAuditLog }) }; }`. The `importOriginal` spread re-exports the `AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS` SSOT through the mocked module so any caller computing `webPageId.slice(0, AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS.TARGET_ID_TRUNCATE_LENGTH)` under the mock continues to work. Block comment cites FIND-IMPL-LCC-PATCH-W5-02 anchor `019df7ab-2f5a` for traceability.
  - `apps/mcp-server/tests/workers/embedding-backfill-worker.test.ts` **+~22 LoC net**: PII-safe logging `it("should truncate webPageId in logs")` block rewritten as 2 `it()` blocks:
    - `it("should truncate webPageId in logs via SSOT-derived TARGET_ID_TRUNCATE_LENGTH", () => { expect(workerSource).toMatch(/webPageId\.slice\(0,\s*AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS\.TARGET_ID_TRUNCATE_LENGTH\)\s*\+\s*"\.\.\."/) })` replaces the previous literal `expect(workerSource).toMatch(/webPageId\.slice\(0, 8\) \+ "\.\.\."/)` assertion (the canonical SSOT-derive surface is now the contract being verified).
    - `it("should import AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS SSOT from audit-log.service", () => { expect(workerSource).toMatch(/AUDIT_LOG_CONSTANTS/); expect(workerSource).toMatch(/from ["']\.\.\/services\/audit-log\.service["']/) })` asserts the import precondition the first test depends on.
    - **Stale-comment cleanup (FIND-WAVE4-V4-FINAL-TDA-L-01 ∩ LCC-L-01)**: the block comment at lines 179-181 referencing "CO-21 carryover: one residual `webPageId.slice(0, 8)` (no `+ "..."`) remains as a job.log() string interpolation (line ~833). Tracked separately as a follow-up — not blocking this contract check." is removed because the residual was closed by the CO-21 migration above; the surviving comment block (lines 170-178) describes only the active Wave 5 canonical contract.

No new test failures introduced by the partial `vi.mock` migrations. `docs:verify` 49/49 PASS preserved (including §15 Quality Gate Enumeration Consistency 27/27 and §16 PII Truncation Literal Regression Gate ADR-0032).

Retains existing `applyPostJobMemoryGate` for the failure path. No `Worker.resume()` callsite remains in this file (CI-verified by INV-WORKER-NO-RESUME-001 source-pin AST assertion).

    - `apps/mcp-server/src/workers/embedding-backfill-worker.ts` — **+75 / −X net (modified)**. Same lifecycle integration pattern as `page-analyze-worker.ts`; reintroduces `_workerInstanceRef` and routes success path through `applyPostJobLifecycleGate`.

Replaces legacy `IPC_SHUTDOWN_GRACE_MS = 2000` with env-overridable IIFE (default 30,000ms; range 1,000-120,000ms; `WORKER_IPC_SHUTDOWN_GRACE_MS`). ### Security / セキュリティ

- **fix(security): SEC pre-release hotfix — CodeQL CWE-829 SRI integrity (test fixtures) + Dependabot hono / @hono/node-server / DOMPurify pnpm.overrides 拡張 (v0.5.0 release/v0.5.0)** / **fix(security): SEC pre-release hotfix — CodeQL CWE-829 SRI integrity (test fixtures) + Dependabot hono / @hono/node-server / DOMPurify pnpm.overrides expansion (v0.5.0 release/v0.5.0)**:
  - **JP**: OSS リポジトリ (TKMD/ReftrixMCP) の Security 監査で 2026-04-29 時点で報告された **2 CodeQL alerts (CWE-829 `js/functionality-from-untrusted-source`)** + **8 Dependabot moderate alerts (hono ×7 + @hono/node-server ×1)** を `release/v0.5.0` ブランチで pre-release 解消。`apps/mcp-server/` 内の test fixture HTML 2 ファイル (`tests/fixtures/js-animations/{gsap-test,anime-js-test}.html`) の cdnjs CDN script 参照に **SRI (Subresource Integrity) `integrity="sha384-..."` + `crossorigin="anonymous"` 属性**を付与し CDN 改竄時の untrusted source 実行を構造的に防止。SHA-384 hash は CDN 配信ファイルから `openssl dgst -sha384 -binary | openssl base64 -A` で算出 (gsap@3.12.2 / anime.js@3.2.1 配信ファイルと bit-exact 一致)。Dependabot は root `package.json` の `pnpm.overrides` を拡張: `hono: ">=4.12.7"` → `">=4.12.16"` (closes 7 GHSA: GHSA-26pp-8wgv-hjvm cookie name validation + GHSA-r5rp-j6wh-rvv4 NBSP prefix bypass + GHSA-458j-xx4x-4375 toSSG path traversal + GHSA-xpcf-pg52-r92g serveStatic middleware bypass + GHSA-wmmm-f939-6g9c JSX HTML injection + GHSA-xf4j-xp2r-rqqx ipRestriction IPv4-mapped + 関連 1 件)、`@hono/node-server: ">=1.19.10"` → `">=1.19.14"` (closes GHSA-92pp-h63x-v22m middleware bypass)、新規 `dompurify: ">=3.4.1"` (defensive override; 主依存は既に 3.4.0 で advisory 対象外だが OSS Dependabot が transitive nested 旧版を検知する可能性に対する深層防御)。Lockfile 解決結果: hono@4.12.7 → 4.12.16、@hono/node-server@1.19.10 → 2.0.1 (latest)、dompurify@3.4.0 → 3.4.1。`pnpm install --lockfile-only` で binary download なしで lockfile 更新。
  - **EN**: Resolves **2 CodeQL alerts (CWE-829 `js/functionality-from-untrusted-source`)** + **8 Dependabot moderate alerts (hono ×7 + @hono/node-server ×1)** reported on the OSS repo (TKMD/ReftrixMCP) at pre-release on the `release/v0.5.0` branch. Added SRI attributes (`integrity="sha384-..."` + `crossorigin="anonymous"`) to the cdnjs CDN script references in 2 `apps/mcp-server/` test fixture HTML files (`tests/fixtures/js-animations/{gsap-test,anime-js-test}.html`), structurally preventing untrusted-source execution if the CDN is tampered with. SHA-384 hashes were computed from CDN-served files via `openssl dgst -sha384 -binary | openssl base64 -A` for bit-exact match against gsap@3.12.2 / anime.js@3.2.1. Dependabot: expanded root `package.json` `pnpm.overrides` — `hono: ">=4.12.7"` → `">=4.12.16"` (closes 7 GHSAs), `@hono/node-server: ">=1.19.10"` → `">=1.19.14"` (closes GHSA-92pp-h63x-v22m), new `dompurify: ">=3.4.1"` (defensive). Lockfile resolution: hono@4.12.7 → 4.12.16, @hono/node-server@1.19.10 → 2.0.1, dompurify@3.4.0 → 3.4.1. Lockfile updated via `pnpm install --lockfile-only`.

- **fix(security): PR-0 SEC hotfix — protobufjs Critical + vite + MCP_API_KEYS 鍵長検証 (CWE-521) (v0.5.0 PR-0) [GHSA-xq3m-2v4x-88gg][GHSA-v2wj-q39q-566r][CWE-521]** / **fix(security): PR-0 SEC hotfix — protobufjs Critical + vite + MCP_API_KEYS key-length check (v0.5.0 PR-0)**:
  - **JP**: PR7e-consolidated 全体 (85 commits, 223 files) を 6 PR に分割する戦略の最優先 PR-0 として、3 件の SEC 防御を `apps/mcp-server/` に landing。
    1. **SEC Critical (GHSA-xq3m-2v4x-88gg)**: `@huggingface/transformers > onnxruntime-web > protobufjs < 7.5.5` の transient Critical 脆弱性をルート `pnpm.overrides` の `protobufjs: >=7.5.5` で強制解消。protobufjs@8.0.1 に pin、`pnpm audit --audit-level=critical` 0 件達成 (本パッケージは依存解決経路の終端としてビルド成果物に影響、SBOM CycloneDX 1.6 driver entry が protobufjs 8.0.1 に sync)。
    2. **SEC High defense-in-depth (GHSA-v2wj-q39q-566r)**: `pnpm.overrides` の `vite: ">=7.3.2 <9"` で vite@8.0.8 に pin。dev-only で exploitable ではないが defense-in-depth として適用、main High 3 件中 2 件解消。
    3. **SEC CWE-521 (M-4a) — `apps/mcp-server/` 直接コード変更**: `apps/mcp-server/src/middleware/auth.ts` に `MIN_ENV_KEY_LENGTH = 40` filter を追加し `MCP_API_KEYS` env 由来のキー長不足を runtime 拒否 (rate-limited warn 出力)。`reftrix_` prefix (8 chars) + 32 chars secret = 40 文字最低要件、190-bit entropy (base62) で 80-bit 安全閾値を大幅超過。`TEST_API_KEY_MAPPINGS` (開発用) は filter 対象外として温存、prod-test 経路の互換性維持。
  - **EN**: As the top-priority PR-0 of the 6-PR split of feat/pr7e-consolidated (85 commits, 223 files), lands 3 SEC defenses into `apps/mcp-server/`.
    1. **SEC Critical (GHSA-xq3m-2v4x-88gg)**: Resolved transient Critical `protobufjs < 7.5.5` (via `@huggingface/transformers > onnxruntime-web > protobufjs`) by pinning `protobufjs: >=7.5.5` (resolved to 8.0.1) in root `pnpm.overrides`. `pnpm audit --audit-level=critical` reports 0 (this package is the leaf consumer; build artefact + CycloneDX 1.6 SBOM driver entry sync to protobufjs@8.0.1).
    2. **SEC High defense-in-depth (GHSA-v2wj-q39q-566r)**: Applied `vite: ">=7.3.2 <9"` (resolved to 8.0.8) as defense-in-depth (not exploitable due to dev-only, no `--host`); resolved 2/3 High findings on main.
    3. **SEC CWE-521 (M-4a) — direct `apps/mcp-server/` code change**: Added `MIN_ENV_KEY_LENGTH = 40` filter in `apps/mcp-server/src/middleware/auth.ts` to runtime-reject under-length `MCP_API_KEYS` env-derived keys (rate-limited warn). `reftrix_` prefix (8 chars) + 32-char secret = 40-char minimum; 190-bit entropy (base62) far exceeds the 80-bit baseline. `TEST_API_KEY_MAPPINGS` (dev keys) is excluded from the filter to preserve prod-test path compatibility.
  - **Per-file LoC deltas / per-file LoC delta**:
    - `apps/mcp-server/src/middleware/auth.ts` **+17/-2 LoC** — `MIN_ENV_KEY_LENGTH = 40` constant + env-key length validation branch + rate-limited warn emission for rejected keys (CWE-521 weak password requirement enforcement at config-load time).
    - `apps/mcp-server/tests/middleware/auth.test.ts` **+80 LoC** — NEW unit cases covering `MIN_ENV_KEY_LENGTH` filter behaviour: env keys ≥40 chars accepted / env keys <40 chars rejected with rate-limited warn / `TEST_API_KEY_MAPPINGS` exempt path / boundary cases (39 chars / 40 chars / 41 chars). 0 `.skip` / `.todo`.

  - **Environment variables / 環境変数**: **No new env var added in `apps/mcp-server/`**. The MIN*ENV_KEY_LENGTH constant is a compile-time SSOT (40 chars, derived from `reftrix*` prefix + 32-char secret entropy budget); not env-tunable per Plan v0.1 §3.1 (intentional non-tunability for security baseline immutability — operator override would lower entropy floor below the 80-bit safety threshold).

  - **JP**: PR-E-1 §1.3 carryover **CO-31 (L, deadline 2026-Q4, pipeline-engineer)** を Item 3 PR で **deadline 前倒し closure**。`apps/mcp-server/src/services/worker-supervisor.service.ts:1260 acquireRedisLockBestEffort` の fail-open path に **retry-with-backoff (100/200/400ms、max 3 attempts、total 700ms bounded)** helper (`tryAcquireLockWithRetry` + `runAcquireLockWithRetryOrchestrator`) を導入。`worker-active-lock.service.ts:236 tryAcquireLock` の **discriminated union API** (PR7d-3 SEC M-1 lineage) を consume し、`reason === "redis_unavailable"` を retry trigger / `reason === "already_held"` を no-retry path として構造的に separate。CO-31 観測 transient Redis failure (production trace 14 分超 page-lock 不在) の degradation window を **14 分超 → ≤700ms に縮小** (NOT elimination per ADR-0011 Amendment 5 §A5.5)。詳細はルート `CHANGELOG.md` および ` 参照。

Consumes `worker-active-lock.service.ts:236 tryAcquireLock` **discriminated union API** (PR7d-3 SEC M-1 lineage), structurally separating `reason === "redis_unavailable"` (retry trigger) vs `reason === "already_held"` (no-retry path). For the CO-31 observed transient Redis failure (production trace: 14+ minute page-lock absence), the degradation window is **reduced from 14+ minutes to ≤700ms** — NOT elimination per ADR-0011 Amendment 5 §A5.5. - **fix(worker): PR-D-9-patch post-merge runtime silent stall regression — IPC schema jobId composite-key acceptance via `BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX` SSOT z.union (v0.4.0 PR-D-9-patch) [EU GDPR Art.5(1)(d)/32(1)(b)][JP APPI 第23条][EU CRA 2024/2847]** / **fix(worker): PR-D-9-patch post-merge runtime silent stall regression — IPC schema jobId composite-key acceptance via `BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX` SSOT z.union (v0.4.0 PR-D-9-patch)**:
  - **JP**: PR-D-9 main 着地後 (stripe.com page.analyze, 2026-04-26-27 時間枠) に backfill drain が再び silent stall する事象を運用観測。原因は PR-D-8 で導入された IPC schema (`worker-ipc.schema.ts`) の `jobId: z.string().uuid().optional()` strict UUID 制約と、PR-D-9 の `EmbeddingBackfillWorker` 経路で `buildBackfillJobId(webPageId, category)` が生成する `<UUID>__<category>` 複合キー間の shape mismatch。Worker child → parent IPC message が `jobId` Zod parse fail → message reject → backfill drain handshake が成立せず production rows が `embedding_backfill_status='pending'` で固定。本 patch は **Option B (z.union + SSOT regex)** で landing。詳細はルート `CHANGELOG.md` を参照。
  - **EN**: After PR-D-9 main merge, backfill drain silent stall regression observed (stripe.com page.analyze, 2026-04-26-27 time window). Root cause: shape mismatch between PR-D-8's IPC schema (`worker-ipc.schema.ts`) `jobId: z.string().uuid().optional()` strict UUID constraint and the `<UUID>__<category>` composite key generated by `buildBackfillJobId(webPageId, category)` in PR-D-9's `EmbeddingBackfillWorker` path. Worker child→parent IPC messages failed Zod parse on `jobId` → message reject → backfill drain handshake never completed → production rows stuck in `embedding_backfill_status='pending'`. This patch lands **Option B (z.union + SSOT regex)**. See root `CHANGELOG.md` for details.
  - **Per-file LoC deltas / per-file LoC delta**:
    - `apps/mcp-server/src/queues/embedding-backfill-queue.ts` **+40 LoC** (`BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX` SSOT export L376-398, RFC 4122 strict-hyphen lowercase + dynamic `EMBEDDING_BACKFILL_CATEGORIES` join, 3-regex doc-comment block L676-691)
    - `apps/mcp-server/src/schemas/worker-ipc.schema.ts` **+22/-1 LoC** (L25 import BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX, L55-72 `jobId` field → `z.union([z.string().uuid(), z.string().regex(BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX)]).optional()` + JSDoc, L110-114 `parseWorkerIpcStrict` cyclomatic delta = 0 inline doc-comment)
    - `apps/mcp-server/src/services/worker-supervisor.service.ts` **0 LoC delta** (1497 unchanged, CO-PRDD9-01 contract STRENGTHENED to absolute neutrality)
    - `apps/mcp-server/tests/regression/standing/worker-lifecycle/inv-worker-lock-003-embedding-backfill-supervisor.test.ts` **+416 LoC net** (Wave 1+2 cases #15-#18 +418 → Wave 4 prettier collapse -2 → Wave 5 case renumber #15-#18 → #20-#23 + 2 pre-existing test bug fixes (vi.doUnmock cross-contamination + hardcoded `"embeddin..."` truncation literal) +57/-23)
  - **Test counts / テスト件数**: Standing regression `worker-lifecycle` 167/167 PASS pass^3 3 consecutive (was 165/167 pre-Wave-5 due to 2 pre-existing test bugs surfaced by case renumber). INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 4 new cases #20-#23 (post-renumber) cross-binding INV-DATA-ACCURACY-001 (GDPR Art.5(1)(d) accuracy invariant literal × 17 occurrences) and INV-WORKER-IPC-SPOOFING (worker-IPC composite-key acceptance verified end-to-end through `parseWorkerIpcStrict` Zod re-validation, preventing IPC payload spoofing on the child→parent boundary; tracked per IO §13 PR-D-9-patch blocking invariant set per TPA-PHASE3-02 M attribution closure).
  - **Quality Gate Pass^3 (corrected) / 品質ゲート Pass^3 (修正版)**: `pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test && pnpm format:check && pnpm docs:verify` — `pnpm test` + `pnpm format:check` を Plan v1.1 §8 enumeration から正式追補 (Plan §13.6 corrected enumeration, FIND-IMPL-TPA-PATCH-W5-01 M / C-IMPL-PATCH-W5-02 resolution per Registry §13.11.5)。
  - **Environment variables / 環境変数**: **none added in this patch** — PR-D-9-patch は schema-side z.union extension のみで、`BACKFILL_JOB_ID_REGEX` は internal SSOT export (env-var configurable ではない、enum SSOT 動的 join により automatic drift tracking)。
  - **6 commits / 6 コミット**: `0d5de99d` (Wave 1+2: SSOT regex + standing test) + `c4c0d3ef` (Wave 3: schema z.union) + `e753400c` (Wave 4: prettier collapse) + `a1d5830d` (chore: docs README format) + `8e040d29` (Wave 5 test: case renumber + 2 bug fixes) + `7337d21b` (Wave 5 doc: Registry §13 prettier fix + §13.11 IO Decision delta).

### Added / 追加

- **feat(worker-supervisor): PR-D-8 extend WorkerSupervisor scope to EmbeddingBackfillWorker (per-type Map + 9 T1 must-fix contracts + 4 audit_logs actions) (v0.4.0 PR-D-8) [EU GDPR Art.30/32(1)(b)][JP APPI 第23条][US CCPA §1798.150][EU CRA 2024/2847]** / **feat(worker-supervisor): PR-D-8 extend WorkerSupervisor scope to EmbeddingBackfillWorker (v0.4.0 PR-D-8)**:
  - **JP**: PR-D-7 empirical verification (linear.app, 2026-04-24) と stripe.com 再現観測 (2026-04-25) で `EmbeddingBackfillWorker` が `WorkerSupervisor` scope 外で稼働している設計 gap を確認。`workerArgs: ["--page"]` ハードコード + `WorkerType="page"` 単一 union により RSS 閾値超過時 self-exit 後の OS-level restart 経路欠落 → production backfill drain が手動介入依存 (GDPR Art.32(1)(b) "ongoing resilience of processing systems" 劣化、APPI 第23条 安全管理措置 scope)。本 PR で `WorkerSupervisor` を **per-type Map 設計 (Option C)** に拡張し、9 T1 Canonical must-fix contracts (Registry v3 §20.1) を landing: per-type child Map + IPC binding table dispatch + 5-rule boot token contract (per-type isolation: `REFTRIX_WORKER_SUPERVISOR_BOOT_TOKEN_PAGE` / `_BACKFILL`) + self-chained respawn 3-layer protocol + 4 audit_logs actions wiring + ESLint complexity gate + `WorkerType` SSOT + IPC Zod schema SSOT + `WorkerChildState` struct + helper module 抽出。Per-type Redis lock key 拡張: `reftrix:worker:active:page` (PageAnalyzeWorker) + `reftrix:worker:active:embedding-backfill` (EmbeddingBackfillWorker)。Staggered scheduling (primary `page` → heartbeat 確立 → secondary `embedding-backfill`) で 32GB tier RSS budget 健全性を担保。MF-V12-01 unified fix で SEC-V12-01 H 🚫 (worker IPC spoofing audit emit) + LCC-V12-01 M 🚫 (Art.30 audit trail completeness) 同時 RESOLVED。MF-V12-02 で standing test 2 cases #13/#14 を新設。**8/8 H findings RESOLVED, 1/1 M finding RESOLVED, accepted-risk 0** (Severity → Landing rule compliant)。LoC re-baseline: Plan v1.0 estimate 350-450 → 1849 measured (1499 supervisor + 350 helpers, +302%) — 9 T1 contracts 同 PR 内 absorption により justified、PR-D-9 class split deferred per CO-20。Legacy `REFTRIX_WORKER_SUPERVISOR_BOOT_TOKEN` deprecated (1-cycle backward compat preserved via fallback、`[DEPRECATION]` warn hard landing は CO-21 deadline 2026-05-31)。詳細はルート `CHANGELOG.md` および ` 参照。
  - **EN**: PR-D-7 empirical verification (linear.app, 2026-04-24) and stripe.com re-reproduction (2026-04-25) confirmed a design gap in which `EmbeddingBackfillWorker` operated **outside `WorkerSupervisor` scope**. Hardcoded `workerArgs: ["--page"]` + single-value `WorkerType="page"` union meant no OS-level restart path post-self-exit, so production backfill drain depended on manual intervention (GDPR Art.32(1)(b) "ongoing resilience of processing systems" degradation; in scope of APPI Article 23 安全管理措置 / safeguarding measures). This PR extends `WorkerSupervisor` to a **per-type Map design (Option C)** and lands 9 T1 Canonical must-fix contracts (Registry v3 §20.1): per-type child Map + IPC binding-table dispatch + 5-rule boot token contract (per-type isolation: `REFTRIX_WORKER_SUPERVISOR_BOOT_TOKEN_PAGE` / `_BACKFILL`) + self-chained respawn 3-layer protocol + 4 `audit_logs` action wiring + ESLint complexity gate + `WorkerType` SSOT + IPC Zod schema SSOT + `WorkerChildState` struct + helper module extraction. Per-type Redis lock keys: `reftrix:worker:active:page` (PageAnalyzeWorker) + `reftrix:worker:active:embedding-backfill` (EmbeddingBackfillWorker). Staggered scheduling (primary `page` start → heartbeat established → secondary `embedding-backfill` start) preserves 32GB tier RSS-budget health. MF-V12-01 unified fix simultaneously resolves SEC-V12-01 H 🚫 (worker IPC spoofing audit emit) + LCC-V12-01 M 🚫 (Art.30 audit-trail completeness). MF-V12-02 lands 2 new standing test cases #13/#14. **8/8 H findings RESOLVED, 1/1 M finding RESOLVED, accepted-risk count 0** (Severity → Landing rule compliant). LoC re-baseline: Plan v1.0 estimate 350-450 → 1849 measured (1499 supervisor + 350 helpers, +302%) — justified by 9 T1 contracts absorbed in-PR; PR-D-9 class split deferred per CO-20 (deadline 2026-06-30). Legacy `REFTRIX_WORKER_SUPERVISOR_BOOT_TOKEN` is deprecated (1-cycle backward compat preserved via fallback; `[DEPRECATION]` warn hard landing tracked in CO-21, deadline 2026-05-31). See root `CHANGELOG.md` and ` for full details.
  - **Per-file LoC deltas / per-file LoC delta** (commit `fc2f733d`, +5291 / -685 across `apps/mcp-server/`):

- **feat(worker+embedding): PR-D-9 EmbeddingBackfillWorker auto-spawn-on-enqueue + responsive backfill handler + parts.visual bbox resolution (v0.4.0 PR-D-9) [EU GDPR Art.5(1)(d)/17/30/32(1)(b)][JP APPI 第23条][US CCPA §1798.150][EU CRA 2024/2847]** / **feat(worker+embedding): PR-D-9 EmbeddingBackfillWorker auto-spawn-on-enqueue + responsive backfill handler + parts.visual bbox resolution (v0.4.0 PR-D-9)**:
  - **JP**: PR-D-8 verify セッションで stripe.com page.analyze (job `019dc8dd-8f84-768e-bf70-5cc56aad95bd`, 568 s, 2026-04-26) において silent stall を観測。`EmbeddingBackfillWorker` が `WorkerSupervisor` 配下で per-type Map に登録されていても、auto-spawn handshake が `analyze.tool.ts` / `batch-analyze.tool.ts` 呼び出し境界で wired されていなかったため、production backfill drain が手動介入依存となっていた。本 PR で 3 領域を統合 landing:
    1. **Auto-spawn handshake (Wave 1)**: `apps/mcp-server/src/tools/page/_shared/worker-bootstrap.ts` (NEW per Plan v1.1 §5.1.17) を `analyze.tool.ts:320` + `batch-analyze.tool.ts:256` から呼び出し、`getWorkerSupervisor().ensureAllWorkersRunningStaggered()` で primary `page` heartbeat 確立 → secondary `embedding-backfill` spawn を staggered 実行。`ENABLE_BACKFILL_AUTOSPAWN` env-var (strict `=== "true"` parse, default = enabled) で fail-safe rollback gate 提供。
    2. **Vision unload precondition (UNB-IMPL-1)**: `apps/mcp-server/src/services/vision/vision-unload-handshake.ts` (NEW, 229 LoC) が `OllamaReadinessProbeService.probe()` 経由で Ollama `/api/ps` を 3s timeout で照会し、Vision residual loaded を検出 → secondary backfill spawn を defer ("vision-loaded" reason + audit_logs emit)。32GB tier RSS budget 健全性 (~10.6GB Vision + ~1.9GB child + ~4GB peak page = ~16.5GB を回避) を担保。Probe failure 時は **fail-closed** (defer + `[SLO_MARKER] vision_probe_failed` warn) — GDPR Art.32(1)(b) "ongoing availability and resilience" 文脈で不確定状態下の進行は availability risk と判定。
    3. **Responsive backfill handler (Wave 3)**: `embedding-backfill.service.ts:1050-1075` (`getMissingResponsiveEmbeddings`) + `:1090-1104` (`countMissingResponsiveEmbeddings`) に `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM web_pages WHERE id = ?::uuid)` 防御節を追加し、GDPR Art.17 `data.delete` 同期削除中の TOCTOU resurrection invariant を保証 (PR2d LCC-M-2 contract 継承)。`ResponsiveProcessor.processInProcess` の `generatedCount:0` 不整合に対し diagnostic log emission + assertion を追加 (PII 配慮: `truncateId(webPageId, 8)` per ` CWE-532)。
    4. **parts.visual bbox resolution (Wave 4)**: `apps/mcp-server/src/services/part/part-bbox-playwright.service.ts` に failure classification (iframe / shadow_dom / dom_disposed / unknown) を追加。`embedding-backfill.service.ts` 配下の `classifyBboxFailure(reason): SkipReasonClassification` private helper (CC delta ≤ +2) で 4-way switch 化し、`bbox_unresolvable` skipReason emit と `audit_logs.embedding_part_visual_skipped` SSOT 経由 (`apps/mcp-server/src/audit/audit-actions.ts`) で routing。

    **Standing regression (UNB-IMPL-2)**:
    - INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 case #15-#19 を `inv-worker-lock-003-embedding-backfill-supervisor.test.ts:1928-2042` に統合 (auto-spawn idempotency + staggered ordering + Conflict-2 audit emit + Vision unload precondition)
    - INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-001 case #1-#6 を `tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-embedding-integrity-001-bbox-and-responsive.test.ts` (NEW, 613 LoC, 10 tests) に landing (responsive missing rows + bbox classification + `bbox_unresolvable` audit emit + GDPR Art.17 TOCTOU mirror + reload budget enforcement)
    - INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 を `inv-schema-enum-004.test.ts` 内で 16-value `EmbeddingSkipReason` exhaustiveness に拡張 (TS-only `as const` SSOT per ADR-0018 §Decision 1 Supplement; Prisma surface 除外 = 3-surface cascade)
    - Integration drain test `tests/integration/page-analyze-backfill-drain.test.ts` (NEW, 293 LoC, 4 tests) を `process.env.CI` gate 経由で landing (fixture URL allowlist 127.0.0.1:38765 + 4 negative SSRF tests, CWE-918)

    **新規環境変数**: `ENABLE_BACKFILL_AUTOSPAWN` (default true, opt-out gate), `BBOX_RESOLVE_RELOAD_ENABLED` (default false, runtime safety), `BBOX_RESOLVE_RELOAD_ENABLED_MAX_RELOADS_PER_PAGE` (default 5), `BBOX_RESOLVE_RELOAD_TOTAL_TIMEOUT_MS` (default 60000)

    **WorkerSupervisor LoC**: 1499 → **1497** (-2 LoC delta, CO-PRDD9-01 contract STRENGTHENED from delta=0; max-lines:1500 ESLint guard cleared)

    **互換性破壊**: なし (Backward compatible API; `ENABLE_BACKFILL_AUTOSPAWN=false` で legacy `ensureWorkerRunning` 経路にロールバック可能)

  - **EN**: PR-D-8 verify session observed silent stall on stripe.com page.analyze (job `019dc8dd-8f84-768e-bf70-5cc56aad95bd`, 568 s, 2026-04-26). Even though `EmbeddingBackfillWorker` was registered per-type under `WorkerSupervisor`, the auto-spawn handshake at the `analyze.tool.ts` / `batch-analyze.tool.ts` invocation boundary was not wired, so production backfill drain depended on manual intervention. This PR consolidates 3 areas:
    1. **Auto-spawn handshake (Wave 1)**: `apps/mcp-server/src/tools/page/_shared/worker-bootstrap.ts` (NEW per Plan v1.1 §5.1.17) is invoked from `analyze.tool.ts:320` + `batch-analyze.tool.ts:256` and calls `getWorkerSupervisor().ensureAllWorkersRunningStaggered()`, which staggers primary `page` heartbeat establishment → secondary `embedding-backfill` spawn. `ENABLE_BACKFILL_AUTOSPAWN` env-var (strict `=== "true"` parse, default = enabled) provides a fail-safe rollback gate.
    2. **Vision unload precondition (UNB-IMPL-1)**: `apps/mcp-server/src/services/vision/vision-unload-handshake.ts` (NEW, 229 LoC) queries Ollama `/api/ps` via `OllamaReadinessProbeService.probe()` with 3s timeout, detects Vision residual loaded → defers secondary backfill spawn ("vision-loaded" reason + `audit_logs` emit). Preserves the 32GB tier RSS budget health (avoiding ~10.6GB Vision + ~1.9GB child + ~4GB peak page = ~16.5GB). On probe failure: **fail-closed** (defer + `[SLO_MARKER] vision_probe_failed` warn) — under GDPR Art.32(1)(b) "ongoing availability and resilience", proceeding under indeterminate state is itself an availability risk.
    3. **Responsive backfill handler (Wave 3)**: Adds the `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM web_pages WHERE id = ?::uuid)` defensive predicate to `embedding-backfill.service.ts:1050-1075` (`getMissingResponsiveEmbeddings`) + `:1090-1104` (`countMissingResponsiveEmbeddings`), guaranteeing the GDPR Art.17 `data.delete` synchronous-deletion TOCTOU resurrection invariant (inheriting the PR2d LCC-M-2 contract). Adds diagnostic log emission + assertion for `ResponsiveProcessor.processInProcess` `generatedCount:0` inconsistency (PII-aware: `truncateId(webPageId, 8)` per ` CWE-532).
    4. **parts.visual bbox resolution (Wave 4)**: Adds failure classification (iframe / shadow_dom / dom_disposed / unknown) to `apps/mcp-server/src/services/part/part-bbox-playwright.service.ts`. The 4-way switch is extracted into the `classifyBboxFailure(reason): SkipReasonClassification` private helper (CC delta ≤ +2) within `embedding-backfill.service.ts`, routing via `bbox_unresolvable` skipReason emit through the `audit_logs.embedding_part_visual_skipped` SSOT (`apps/mcp-server/src/audit/audit-actions.ts`).

    **Standing regression (UNB-IMPL-2)**:
    - INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 case #15-#19 consolidated into `inv-worker-lock-003-embedding-backfill-supervisor.test.ts:1928-2042` (auto-spawn idempotency + staggered ordering + Conflict-2 audit emit + Vision unload precondition)
    - INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-001 case #1-#6 landed in `tests/regression/standing/large-page/inv-embedding-integrity-001-bbox-and-responsive.test.ts` (NEW, 613 LoC, 10 tests; responsive missing rows + bbox classification + `bbox_unresolvable` audit emit + GDPR Art.17 TOCTOU mirror + reload budget enforcement)
    - INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 extended within `inv-schema-enum-004.test.ts` for 16-value `EmbeddingSkipReason` exhaustiveness (TS-only `as const` SSOT per ADR-0018 §Decision 1 Supplement; Prisma surface excluded = 3-surface cascade)
    - Integration drain test `tests/integration/page-analyze-backfill-drain.test.ts` (NEW, 293 LoC, 4 tests) landed via `process.env.CI` gate (fixture URL allowlist 127.0.0.1:38765 + 4 negative SSRF tests, CWE-918)

    **New environment variables**: `ENABLE_BACKFILL_AUTOSPAWN` (default true, opt-out gate), `BBOX_RESOLVE_RELOAD_ENABLED` (default false, runtime safety), `BBOX_RESOLVE_RELOAD_ENABLED_MAX_RELOADS_PER_PAGE` (default 5), `BBOX_RESOLVE_RELOAD_TOTAL_TIMEOUT_MS` (default 60000)

    **WorkerSupervisor LoC**: 1499 → **1497** (-2 LoC delta; CO-PRDD9-01 contract STRENGTHENED from delta=0; max-lines:1500 ESLint guard cleared)

    **Backward compatibility**: Preserved (Backward compatible API; rollback to legacy `ensureWorkerRunning` path via `ENABLE_BACKFILL_AUTOSPAWN=false`)

  - **Related contracts / 関連契約**: ADR-0011 Amendment 2 (Vision unload precondition handshake at `apps/mcp-server/src/services/vision/ollama-readiness-probe.ts` + 7-component RSS budget table at line 403-576); ADR-0018 §Decision 1 Supplement (EmbeddingSkipReason TS-only `as const` SSOT extension to 16 values + `audit_logs.action` SSOT constants per `apps/mcp-server/src/audit/audit-actions.ts` at line 187+); INV-WORKER-LOCK-003 (5 new cases #15-#19); INV-EMBEDDING-INTEGRITY-001 (6 new cases #1-#6); INV-SCHEMA-ENUM-004 (16-value EmbeddingSkipReason exhaustiveness); INV-DATA-DELETE-002 (cross-tag for case #5 GDPR Art.17 TOCTOU)
